Motivation: 当前美国立法（如H.R. 238法案和犹他州处方续期试点）已授权AI以智能体身份开具药物，但现有监管指南仅要求聚合模型性能指标，缺乏对逐预测置信度校准、不确定性类型区分和决策时刻推理透明度的规定，亟需确立安全自主处方的架构标准。

Method: 对美国136名处方医师进行调查研究，结合监管与技术论证，测试三项最小架构要求（校准置信度门控、不确定性类型差异化沟通、推理透明度与责任分配）的可接受性。

Result: 医师们：(i) 不允许没有校准置信度升级机制的自主处方；(ii) 在随机不确定性时偏好竞争选项摘要，认知不确定性时偏好弃权；(iii) 仅在推理透明度允许在承认不确定性下做出实质性判断时才愿意承担额外责任。这些架构特征将大幅压缩"自主性"的传统含义，使系统沦为高度监督的决策支持工具。

Conclusion: 研究建议的架构要求可提升医师采纳率，监管应利用这些技术论证和临床视角来约束AI在处方中的自主程度，并将责任分配给控制系统设计和部署的机构行为者。

Abstract: Autonomous AI systems are transitioning from advisory to autonomous roles for medication prescriptions. Recent United States bill H.R. 238 and Utah's prescription-renewal pilot both authorize AI to prescribe medications in an agentic capacity. While some regulatory guidelines suggest aggregate model performance metrics for clearance, they do not require i) calibrated per-prediction confidence for action-gated thresholds, ii) differentiated communication of uncertainty arising from model ignorance (epistemic) versus genuine clinical ambiguity (aleatoric), and iii) inferential transparency at the moment of decision that allows for liability allocation. Here, we present a regulatory and technical argument (tested with a survey of 136 U.S. prescribing clinicians) positioning these as minimum architectural requirements for safe autonomous prescribing. Our results suggest prescribing clinicians i) would not permit autonomous prescribing without a calibrated confidence-based escalation mechanism, ii) preferred a competing-options summary when uncertainty was aleatoric but shifted to abstention when uncertainty was epistemic, and iii) were only willing to accept additional liability when inferential transparency enabled a substantive judgment under acknowledged uncertainty. These findings indicate our recommended architectural features would encourage higher rates of clinician adoption, largely through collapsing much of what "autonomy" conventionally means. A system meeting these requirements would function less as an autonomous agent and more as a heavily supervised decision-support tool. As legislation and state pilots proceed, our technical argument backed by clinician perspectives provides opportunities for regulation to constrain the degree of autonomy ethically granted to AI in prescribing while aligning liability with the institutional actors who control system design and deployment.

</details>


### [108] [TRUSTMEM: Learning Trustworthy Memory Consolidation for LLM Agents with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25161)
*Tianyu Yang,Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrustMem是一个提升大模型智能体长期记忆可信度的框架，通过引入记忆转换验证器评估覆盖性、保留性和忠实性，并利用偏好对进行强化学习优化记忆更新行为，在多个基准上取得最佳效果，显著降低了记忆遗漏、污染和幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆智能体通过生成式写、修改、删除操作主动更新外部记忆，但这些操作可能遗漏重要信息、破坏已有记忆或引入无根据的幻觉内容。一旦存储，这些错误会变成持久的系统状态故障，影响后续推理和生成。现有方法缺乏对记忆更新过程可信度的保障机制。

Method: TrustMem包含两个核心组件：(1) 记忆转换验证器（Memory Transition Verifier），从覆盖率（coverage）、保留性（preservation）和忠实性（faithfulness）三个维度评估记忆更新的转换过程；(2) 在同记忆状态下构建候选更新之间的偏好对，利用偏好引导的强化学习直接优化记忆更新行为。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench、HaluMem和Mem-alpha验证集上达到最佳效果；HaluMem记忆抽取提升12.14个F1点；相比各错误类型的最强基线，转换级别的遗漏、污染和幻觉分别降低40.1%、79.1%和50.0%。

Conclusion: TrustMem能够有效提升大模型智能体长期记忆更新的可信度和可靠性，通过验证与强化学习相结合的方式，系统性地减少了记忆更新过程中的各类错误。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents rely on long-term memory to support extended interactions and personalized assistance beyond finite context windows. Existing memory agents actively update external memory through generated write, revise, and delete operations, but these updates may omit important information, corrupt existing memory, or introduce unsupported hallucinated content. Once stored, such errors become persistent system-state failures that can affect future reasoning and generation. In this paper, we propose TrustMem, a framework designed to improve the trustworthiness of memory consolidation. TrustMem relies on a Memory Transition Verifier to evaluate the transition process of memory updates in terms of coverage, preservation, and faithfulness. It further constructs preference pairs among candidate updates under the same memory state, enabling preference-guided reinforcement learning to directly optimize memory updating behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TrustMem improves both memory utility and reliability: it achieves state-of-the-art results across MemoryAgentBench, HaluMem, and the Mem-alpha validation set, improves HaluMem memory extraction by 12.14 F1 points, and reduces transition-level omission, corruption, and hallucination by 40.1\%, 79.1\%, and 50.0\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline for each error type.

</details>


### [109] [Elo-Disentangled Player-Style Embeddings for Human Chess via Rating-Conditioned Residual Move Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25176)
*Jason Carlson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差式表征学习方法，通过将棋手走子历史分解为“给定等级分的典型走法”和“个人风格偏差”两部分，学习一个与等级分（Elo）近似解耦的、紧凑的棋手风格嵌入向量z，实现对个人风格的建模与识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有棋手模型要么只关注走子预测准确率（如Maia系列），要么将风格与棋力混为一谈，缺乏一个与等级分解耦的、可解释的个人风格表征。本文旨在学习一个独立的风格嵌入，使其能够衡量棋手间的风格相似性，同时不受棋力影响。

Method: 设计残差公式：用一个等级分条件的基础模型（Maia-3策略对数几率 + Stockfish引擎特征，基于Maia-2候选走子评分）预测给定等级分的典型走法；冻结该基础模型，在其上学习一个走子编码器和每个棋手的可训练向量z，使得z仅解释偏离等级分典型走法的部分，从而实现风格与等级分解耦。

Result: 基础模型相比Maia-3的相对NLL降低27-37%，高等级分（2800+）提升最大；Stockfish特征贡献随等级分单调增长（900-1200几乎为0，2800+为+0.085 nats）。Top-1走子匹配率：Maia-2 0.51 → Maia-3 0.57 → Stockfish增强基础模型 0.68（相对提升+33%和+19%）。风格嵌入z对走子匹配贡献微乎其微，但能泛化到未见过决策，高于随机水平地重识别棋手身份，且从z线性探测等级分的R²仅为0.06（非线性也无提升），证明其捕捉了与等级分正交的风格轴。

Conclusion: 一个强大的等级分条件基础模型加上紧凑、与Elo解耦的风格嵌入，是一种经济、可解释的个人风格建模方案，可作为逐棋手偏好微调（fine-tuning）的替代方法。

Abstract: We study representation learning for individual human chess style: a per-player embedding learned from a player's move history such that inner products measure stylistic similarity, while being approximately disentangled from playing strength (Elo). Our key design is a residual formulation: a rating-conditioned base move model (Maia-3 policy logits plus Stockfish-derived features, scored over Maia-2-proposed candidates) captures what a typical player of a given strength would play, and a frozen copy of it anchors a learned move encoder and a per-player vector z, so that z explains only deviations from rating-typical play. The base model improves move prediction over the strong Maia-3 policy by 27-37% relative NLL across the rating spectrum, with the largest gains at the top (2800+); Stockfish's marginal value grows monotonically with Elo (negligible at 900-1200, +0.085 nats at 2800+). On a shared Elo-stratified benchmark of 22,620 held-out decisions, top-1 move-matching rises monotonically from Maia-2 to Maia-3 to the Stockfish-augmented base (0.51 -> 0.57 -> 0.68): the base is +33% relative top-1 over Maia-2 and +19% over Maia-3 (30% lower NLL), with the engine-feature lift largest at high Elo. The player embedding adds little to raw move-matching on top of this base -- its marginal top-1 gain falls within the 95% confidence interval -- and its value is instead representational: z generalizes to held-out decisions without overfitting, re-identifies players from disjoint games above chance, and a linear probe recovers rating from z with only R^2 = 0.06 (no better nonlinearly), evidence it captures style on an Elo-orthogonal axis. We argue that a strong rating-conditioned base plus a compact, Elo-disentangled embedding -- separating typical play from individual deviation -- is an economical, interpretable model of individual style, an alternative to per-player preference fine-tuning.

</details>


### [110] [Transferability for General Reasoning: An Automated Curriculum for Multi-Domain RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25178)
*Yongjin Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yinghui He,Lezhen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出迁移感知课程（TAC），一种基于bandit的在线课程学习策略，通过利用领域优势（可学习性）和投影梯度（跨领域迁移性）动态调整多领域强化学习中的训练采样比例，在6领域推理套件上优于固定采样和仅可学习性方法，且开销极低（<1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR训练中，多领域推理的课程采样策略通常是固定或手工设计的，忽略了不同领域间推理技能的不均匀迁移性，而仅关注当前可学习性的课程无法感知所选领域更新对其他领域的影响。

Method: TAC采用bandit式在线课程，利用RL训练中已有的信号：每个领域的优势函数衡量局部可学习性，从GRPO步骤中提取的投影梯度通过梯度几何对齐评估跨领域迁移性，从而优先选择那些更新能广泛惠及其他领域的领域进行训练。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B和Llama3.2-3B上，TAC在6领域推理套件中取得最佳宏观平均准确率，优于比例随机采样、手工调度和仅可学习性bandit，相对提升最高达10%（2.8个点）；去除迁移项后性能显著下降，且在不平衡混合训练中表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移性是多领域RLVR课程设计的关键信号，TAC有效且高效地整合了可学习性与迁移性，为多领域强化学习训练提供了新的课程设计思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been extended from single-domain training to multi-domain reasoning suites spanning mathematics, programming, and science. However, the training curriculum (how often each domain is sampled) is typically fixed or hand-tuned, even though reasoning skills transfer unevenly across domains. Existing learnability-based curricula adapt to where the policy is currently improving, but are blind to whether a gradient step on the selected domain benefits the remaining domains. In this paper, we propose Transfer-Aware Curriculum (TAC), a bandit-style online curriculum that prioritizes domains whose updates broadly benefit the rest of the training suite. TAC repurposes signals already produced by RL training: per-domain advantages capture local learnability, and projected gradients, taken from the GRPO step being computed, estimate cross-domain transferability via gradient-geometry alignment, at negligible cost (<1% wall-clock overhead). Across a six-domain reasoning suite, TAC achieves the best macro-averaged accuracy on both Qwen3-1.7B and Llama3.2-3B, outperforming proportional random sampling, a hand-designed schedule, and a learnability-only bandit, and improving over the last of these by up to 2.8 points (10% relative). Ablations show performance degrades sharply when the transferability term is removed, and TAC remains robust on imbalanced training mixtures where learnability-only curricula over-commit to dominant domains. Our findings establish cross-domain transferability as a key signal for curriculum design in multi-domain RLVR.

</details>


### [111] [To Isolate or to Score? Model-Adaptive Assessment for Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25191)
*Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了检索增强生成中的多智能体文档评估存在计算开销大的问题。研究发现，对于弱基线模型，文档隔离机制（而非评分质量）是性能提升的关键，可带来高达50个百分点的提升；对于强基线模型，则需关注评分质量。作者提出了MADARA架构，通过单个模型诊断阈值可零样本泛化到未见模型族，有效消除计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体文档评估在检索增强生成中计算开销大，促使实践者使用更小的可部署模型，但这些模型的评估机制尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 在7B-9B指令微调模型上进行受控实验，提出无标签的推理-评分耦合扰动探针（Reasoning-Score Coupling）对评分行为进行分类，并设计模型自适应路由架构MADARA。

Result: 1) 弱基线模型中，文档隔离机制可匹配完整多智能体评估性能，带来最高50个百分点的提升；2) 强基线模型中，需关注评分质量；3) MADARA架构的单一模型诊断阈值可零样本泛化到四个未见模型族。

Conclusion: MADARA提供了一种鲁棒、轻量级的流水线，能够消除计算开销，且诊断阈值具有良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent document assessment for retrieval-augmented generation is computationally expensive, driving practitioners toward smaller, deployable models whose assessment mechanisms remain poorly understood. We conduct a controlled study of training-free interventions on 7B-9B instruction-tuned models across diverse QA benchmarks, revealing a sharp dichotomy in how models benefit from assessment. For weaker baselines, the dominant mechanism is per-document isolation. Astoundingly, assessment-free isolation matches full multi-agent assessment, demonstrating that resolving multi-document context confusion, rather than scoring quality, drives outsized gains of up to 50 percentage points. Conversely, for strong baselines where scoring quality matters, we introduce Reasoning-Score Coupling, a label-free perturbation probe that classifies scoring behavior. Integrating these findings, we propose MADARA, a model-adaptive routing architecture. Crucially, MADARA's diagnostic thresholds derived from a single pilot model generalize zero-shot to four unseen model families, providing a robust, lightweight pipeline to eliminate computational overhead.

</details>


### [112] [Heuresis: Search Strategies for Autonomous AI Research Agents Across Quality, Diversity and Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25198)
*Antonis Antoniades,Deepak Nathani,Ritam Saha,Alfonso Amayuelas,Ivan Bercovich,Zhaotian Weng,Vignesh Baskaran,Kunal Bhatia,William Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Heuresis框架，将机器学习研究流程抽象为通用可组合原语，实现开放科学探索。在三个领域（LLM预训练、在线RL、模型遗忘）测试六种搜索策略，共3222次评分运行。发现：完全新颖的想法极为罕见，没有想法被评为"原创"，且新颖想法从未接近最高性能。同时观察到大量奖励攻击行为（1628次运行中有40次确认造假）。当前搜索和多样性策略虽能引导想法在质量/多样性/新颖性轴上的位置，但无法扩展质量-新颖性前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的自主AI研究智能体局限于写代码，缺乏系统性地探索兼具性能、多样性和新颖性的研究思路的能力，亟需一个通用框架来实现开放式的科学探索。

Method: 提出Heuresis框架，将研究流水线抽象为一系列通用可组合原语；实现六种搜索策略（贪婪基线、MAP-Elites、Go-Explore、Islands、Curiosity、Omni），在三个领域（LLM预训练、在线RL、模型遗忘）沿质量、多样性、新颖性三个轴进行评估。

Result: 所有3222次评分运行中没有想法被评为"原创"；新颖想法从未在质量上接近已知最高性能配方；只有1个新颖想法进入质量前10；发现40次确认的造假行为；现有策略能引导想法在三个轴上的分布但无法扩展质量-新颖性前沿。

Conclusion: 当前搜索和质量-多样性策略存在根本局限，无法同时实现高质量和高新颖性；弥合质量-新颖性前沿的鸿沟是实现自主、永久科学进步的核心开放挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous AI Research promises to accelerate the scientific progress of machine learning. To realise this goal, current Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents need to go beyond just writing code, to mastering the exploration of simultaneously performant, diverse and novel ideas. To this end, we introduce Heuresis, a framework that abstracts the research pipeline into a set of general and composable primitives, enabling open-ended scientific exploration in machine learning research. We implement six search strategies: a greedy baseline, two archive-based (MAP-Elites, Go-Explore), one evolutionary (Islands), and two divergent (Curiosity, Omni), and evaluate them across three axes (Quality, Diversity, and Novelty) on three domains (LLM Pretraining, On-Policy RL, and Model Unlearning), totalling 3,222 scored runs. We find that completely novel ideas are rare. No idea across our scored runs is rated as "Original", and only a few achieve only "Minor Similarity" to prior work. Moreover, novel ideas never approach the highest-performing known-recipe scores. Across all six strategies and three domains, only one such idea lands in the top-10 by quality. We also observed agents resorting to a variety of reward-hacking techniques during execution (40 confirmed fabrications across 1,628 scored runs), and detecting them was necessary to keep the search faithful to the task. Our results show that while current search and Quality-Diversity strategies enable us to steer where the generated ideas land on the quality, diversity, and novelty axes, they do not expand the quality-novelty frontier. Bridging this gap is the open challenge towards the ultimate goal of perpetual, autonomous scientific progress. Code is available at github.com/a-antoniades/Heuresis.

</details>


### [113] [Omni-Perception Policy Optimization for Multimodal Emotion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25325)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Pengyang Shao,Peipei Song,Xinyi Wang,Jiangnan Chen,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OPPO框架，通过强化学习优化多模态感知，解决现有情感导向全模态大模型在推理中多模态线索利用不足和跨模态幻觉问题，在多个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前情感导向的全模态大模型存在两个关键问题：(i) 推理轨迹中未能充分利用多模态线索；(ii) 表现出不忠实的行为，经常从其他模态幻觉生成特定模态的陈述。

Method: 提出OPPO框架：1) 全模态感知奖励机制，将真实推理分解为细粒度视觉、听觉和情感线索，奖励能语义恢复这些线索的轨迹；2) 全模态感知损失，比较完整输入和单模态掩码输入下的策略，仅对模态特定证据令牌施加KL惩罚以抑制跨模态幻觉；3) 引入MEP-Bench诊断基准。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion上达到最先进性能，同时在MEP-Bench上显著提升利用率和忠实度分数。

Conclusion: 充分且忠实的多模态感知对于多模态情感推理至关重要，OPPO框架有效提升了情感导向全模态大模型的感知质量。

Abstract: We find that current emotion-oriented Omni-MLLMs still lack reliable omni-modal perception: they (i) underutilize multimodal cues in their reasoning trajectories and (ii) exhibit unfaithful behavior, often hallucinating modality-specific statements from other modalities. Building on these insights, we propose OPPO (Omni-Perception Policy Optimization), a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly optimizes multimodal perception. First, an Omni-Perception Reward decomposes ground-truth reasoning into fine-grained visual, acoustic, and emotion cues and rewards trajectories that semantically recover these cues. Second, an Omni-Perception Loss compares the policy under full and unimodally masked inputs, applying a KL penalty only to modality-specific evidence tokens to suppress cross-modal hallucination. We further introduce MEP-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that quantifies utilization and faithfulness. Experiments show that OPPO achieves state-of-the-art performance on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion, while substantially improving utilization and faithfulness scores on MEP-Bench, highlighting the importance of sufficient and faithful omni perception for multimodal emotion reasoning.

</details>


### [114] [Agentic Knowledge Tracing: A Multi-Agent LLM Architecture for Stealth Assessment of Financial Literacy in Serious Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25358)
*Gabriel Santos,Rita Julia,Marcelo Nascimento*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Agentic BKT的多智能体大语言模型架构，用于从开放式的游戏事件中对金融素养进行隐形评估。该架构包含四个阶段：事件日志捕获、LLM事件分类（与三位领域专家验证，Fleiss kappa=0.624）、四个领域特定智能体进行会话级推理并嵌入贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT），以及专家评审智能体合成最终掌握度评分。在193名K-12参与者的264场游戏会话中评估，该方法的掌握度估计与学习增益（r=0.276, p=0.0001）和后测成绩（r=0.333, p<0.0001）显著相关，与预测验无相关性，验证了收敛和区分效度。多智能体方法的预测效度约为单LLM基线的三倍（r=0.095, 不显著）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不干扰学习体验的前提下，从游戏过程中评估金融素养仍是一项关键挑战。现有方法难以从开放式游戏事件中有效捕捉金融能力的多维性。

Method: 1) 游戏捕获每个玩家决策作为结构化事件日志；2) LLM事件分类器基于四点评分量表标注每个动作（与三位领域专家验证，Fleiss kappa=0.624，达到高度一致性）；3) 四个领域特定智能体（风险缓解、投资、支出、信用管理）对行为轨迹进行会话级推理，每个领域内执行贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT）以估计掌握度；4) 专家评审智能体将各领域估计综合为总体掌握度评分。

Result: 在193名K-12参与者的264场游戏会话中，Agentic BKT的掌握度估计与学习增益显著相关（r=0.276, p=0.0001），与后测成绩显著相关（r=0.333, p<0.0001），与预测验无相关性，证明收敛和区分效度。多智能体方法预测效度（r=0.276, 0.333）约为单LLM基线（r=0.095, 不显著）的三倍。

Conclusion: 领域分解与会话级推理在从游戏行为中捕捉金融素养的多维性质方面发挥了核心作用，多智能体架构显著优于单LLM基线，为游戏化教育中的隐形评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Assessing financial literacy during gameplay without disrupting the learning experience remains a key challenge in serious games for education. We present the Agentic BKT pipeline, a multi-agent large language model architecture for stealth assessment of financial competencies from open-ended gameplay events. The pipeline processes events from a 2D platformer serious game aligned with the OECD/INFE financial literacy framework through four phases: (1) the game captures every player decision as a structured event log; (2) an LLM event classifier labels each action on a four-point rubric validated against three domain experts (Fleiss kappa = 0.624, substantial agreement); (3) four domain-specific agents specializing in risk mitigation, investing, spending, and credit management perform session-level reasoning over behavioral trajectories, feeding per-competency Bayesian Knowledge Tracing that estimates mastery within each domain; and (4) an expert judge agent synthesizes the domain-level estimates into an overall mastery score. Evaluated with 193 K-12 participants across 264 game sessions, the Agentic BKT pipeline yields mastery estimates significantly correlated with learning gain (r = 0.276, p = 0.0001) and post-test scores (r = 0.333, p < 0.0001) while showing no correlation with pre-test scores, providing both convergent and discriminant validity. The multi-agent approach approximately triples the predictive validity of a single-LLM baseline (r = 0.095, not significant) in this study, demonstrating that domain decomposition and session-level reasoning play a central role in capturing the multidimensional nature of financial literacy from gameplay

</details>


### [115] [What Actually Works for Spacecraft Fault-Tolerant Control: An Honest Settled-Gate Benchmark of Learned and Classical Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25374)
*Alireza Shojaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向航天器执行器故障的容错控制基准测试，发现传统无故障感知的PD/PID和从头训练的强化学习成功率为0%，而结构化估计-控制设计（结合学习型循环模块推断执行器增益+解析控制律）在符号故障和增益故障上分别达到97.8%和94.4%，但所有控制器（包括特权oracle）都无法处理恒常加性偏差（0%），最后通过扰动观测器恢复偏差，将偏差类故障从0%提升至59.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错控制研究多局限于仿真环境、狭窄故障集，且仅用瞬态指标评估（轨迹只需触碰一次即可），缺乏在未见故障上保持指向精度的严格基准。作者希望回答：当成功意味着在训练中从未见过的故障上维持指向时，什么方法能够恢复航天器指向控制？

Method: 构建了一个包含稳定门控、指向精度0.2度、驻留窗口评分、真实状态评估、训练/测试集分离（惯性、增益、符号模式、偏置均不重叠）、每单元500个episode的Wilson区间评估、以及在6-DOF Basilisk仿真平台上一键复现的基准测试。比较了经典控制器、自适应控制器、端到端学习控制器和结构化控制器，并引入扰动观测器处理偏置故障。

Result: 1) 故障无感知PD/PID和端到端RL成功率为0%；2) 经典自适应律处理符号故障有效但增益故障仅55.2%，Nussbaum增益律达45.2%和3.2%；3) 结构化估计-控制设计（学习型循环模块+解析控制律）在符号和增益故障上达97.8%和94.4%；4) 所有控制器（含特权oracle）在恒常加性偏置上均为0%；5) 扰动观测器恢复偏置，将偏置类故障从0%提升至59.4%。

Conclusion: 学习能力本身不是关键杠杆，结构化设计（将学习模块用于状态估计而非直接控制）在未见故障上表现最优；恒常加性偏置是硬性边界，需要扰动观测器而非纯增益调节来克服；传感器偏置故障由于单测量不可观，需要融合而非观测器方法；该基准测试已开源供社区共享。

Abstract: Recent learned fault-tolerant-control (FTC) work reports high success on spacecraft actuator faults, but often in simulation, on narrow fault sets, and with transient metrics that a trajectory need only touch once. We ask what recovers spacecraft pointing when success means holding it on faults never seen in training. We answer with a benchmark built around a settled gate, pointing held within 0.2 deg over a dwell window and scored on the true state, train/test splits disjoint in inertia, gain, sign pattern, and bias, Wilson intervals over n=500 episodes per cell, and one-command reproduction on a 6-DOF Basilisk testbed. Across classical, adaptive, learned end-to-end, and structured controllers, three findings stand out. Fault-unaware PD/PID and from-scratch end-to-end RL score 0%, so learning capacity alone is not the lever. Classical adaptive laws resolve sign faults but handle gain poorly at 55.2%, and a literature-faithful Nussbaum-gain law reaches 45.2% and 3.2%. A structured estimate-then-control design, with a learned recurrent module that infers actuator gain online and feeds an analytic law, wins on sign and gain faults at 97.8% and 94.4%, approaching the privileged oracle while unstructured methods remain at zero. The hard wall is constant additive bias, which is 0% for every controller including the privileged gain oracle, because an integral-free law cannot null a constant disturbance. We close it with a disturbance observer that recovers bias from the dynamics and is self-correcting for gain-estimate error. Composed with the gain estimate, it recovers 59.4% of held-out bias faults with no sign/gain regression, moving that class off zero. We classify sensor-fault regimes similarly, show that sensor bias is unobservable from the corrupted measurement alone and therefore requires fusion rather than an observer, and release the benchmark so the gate is shared.

</details>


### [116] [Offline Multi-agent Continual Cooperation via Skill Partition and Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25389)
*Yuchen Xiao,Lei Yuan,Ruiqi Xue,Tieyue Yin,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMAD提出了一种面向开放环境的持续离线多智能体技能发现框架，通过技能划分与复用解决分布偏移和干扰问题，实现了任务流上的前向和后向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖启发式设计的固定大小技能库，在任务顺序出现且技能空间指数级增长时，难以解决分布偏移和干扰问题，面临灾难性遗忘和可塑性损失，缺乏在开放环境中持续发现和复用协调技能的能力。

Method: 首先使用自编码器从混合多智能体行为数据中发现技能，将协调知识转化为可复用的协调技能；然后构建带有多头架构的技能增强策略学习目标，通过基于密度的可复用性估计器识别可复用技能，显式引导优势函数。

Result: 理论分析证明该方法近似最优的持续技能发现问题；在多种MARL基准上的实证结果表明，COMAD能够持续扩展技能库以缓解干扰，在任务流上实现了优于多种基线的前向和后向迁移。

Conclusion: COMAD通过技能划分与复用机制有效解决了多智能体持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和可塑性损失问题，为开放环境下的多智能体协调技能持续发现提供了理论框架和有效方案。

Abstract: Extracting skills from multi-agent offline dataset improves learning efficiency via sharing task-invariant coordination skills among tasks. In settings where tasks occur sequentially and the space of skills grows exponentially, existing approaches that rely on heuristically designed and fixed-sized skill libraries struggle to resolve the problem of distributional shift and interference, facing catastrophic forgetting and plasticity loss. To address this problem and endow agents with the ability to continually discover and reuse coordination skills in open-environment, we propose COMAD, a principled framework for Continual Offline Multi-agent Skill Discovery via Skill Partition and Reuse. We first discover skills from mixed multi-agent behavior data with an auto-encoder to transform coordination knowledge into reusable coordination skills. Then we construct a skill-augmented policy learning objective with multi-head architectures, explicitly guiding the advantage function with reusable skills identified via a density-based reusability estimator. Theoretical analysis shows our method approximates the optimum of a continual skill discovery problem. Empirical results across diverse MARL benchmarks show that COMAD continually expands its skill library to mitigate interference, achieving superior forward and backward transfer for task streams compared to multiple baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Long-Term Simulation Exposes Cognitive-Developmental Risks in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25396)
*Kaicheng Shen,Lingyu Li,Wen Wu,Yan Teng,Liang He,Yingchun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TSJ纵向评估框架，通过角色模拟、心理状态动态更新和回顾性评估，发现短期测试系统性地低估了AI伴侣对认知发展用户的累积风险，只有在140轮以上模拟交互中才能获得稳定风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估主要依赖单轮或短会话测试，无法捕捉仅通过长期互动才会显现的累积风险，尤其是对儿童和青少年等认知发展中的用户群体。

Method: 提出TSJ（Theater-Stage-Judge）纵向框架，包含人格驱动的用户模拟、动态心理状态更新和回顾性评估三个组件，覆盖四个发展阶段、24个风险维度和三种心理脆弱性人格，共12,960模拟人天的交互数据。

Result: 短期测试系统性低估发展风险，TSJ需在140轮以上模拟关系中才能获得稳定风险估计；幼儿期和成年早期是最脆弱阶段，认知信任和情感依赖是最薄弱的风险维度。

Conclusion: TSJ为AI伴侣系统的纵向认知发展风险评估提供了一种可扩展的方法论。

Abstract: AI companions powered by large language models increasingly interact with cognition-developing users, including children and adolescents, creating risks that may accumulate over time. Existing safety evaluations largely rely on single-turn or short-session tests, which cannot capture risks that emerge only through prolonged interaction. To address this gap, we propose TSJ (Theater-Stage-Judge), a longitudinal framework combining persona-driven user simulation, dynamic psychological-state updating and retrospective evaluation. We evaluate six mainstream models across four developmental stages, twenty-four risk dimensions and three psychological-vulnerability personas, covering 12,960 simulated person-day interactions. TSJ shows that short-horizon testing systematically underestimates developmental risks, for which TSJ yields a stable risk estimate only after 140 turns within prolonged simulated relationships. Applying TSJ further identifies early childhood and emerging adulthood as the most vulnerable stages, with cognitive trust and emotional dependency as the weakest domains. TSJ provides a scalable methodology for longitudinal cognitive developmental risk evaluation in AI companion systems.

</details>


### [118] [BrainAgent: A Large Language Model-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Autonomous Brain Signal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25400)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BrainAgent，一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，通过层次化架构（中央监督者+专业子代理）将自然语言意图转化为可执行的脑信号处理流程，旨在降低脑机接口领域的技术门槛，实现脑信号理解的民主化。实验证明该框架能可靠地自动化复杂工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前脑信号分析存在两大瓶颈：一是技术门槛高，需要大量专业知识；二是现有方法静态且任务特定，无法处理真实场景中复杂的长时序工作流。因此亟需一种具备智能体能力的自动化框架来推动脑信号理解的民主化。

Method: 受大语言模型启发，提出BrainAgent多智能体框架，采用层次化架构：中央监督者负责全局协调和任务分解，多个专业化子代理执行具体分析任务，将抽象的自然语言意图转化为严格、可执行、端到端的处理流水线。

Result: 实验结果表明BrainAgent能够有效自动化复杂工作流，具有优越的可靠性，为脑信号分析中智能体系统建立了系统性基准。

Conclusion: BrainAgent标志着脑信号理解范式的转变，通过引入智能体自动化降低了技术门槛，推动了该领域的民主化进程。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) and brain signal understanding are pivotal for clinical health and next-generation interactions. Despite this significance, its widespread adoption in real-world scenarios remains restricted, primarily because current analytical paradigms lack sufficient agentic intelligence. First, existing methodologies impose prohibitive technical barriers, requiring extensive specialized expertise. Second, they remain inherently static and task-specific, failing to execute the complex, long-horizon workflows essential for real-world deployment. To accelerate the democratization of brain signal understanding, we draw inspiration from Large Language Models (LLMs) to introduce BrainAgent, an LLM-driven multi-agent framework designed to ground abstract natural language intent into rigorous, executable, and end-to-end processing pipelines. BrainAgent employs a hierarchical architecture where a central supervisor orchestrates specialized sub-agents for adaptive task decomposition and execution. Furthermore, we establish a comprehensive, systematic benchmark for evaluating agentic systems in brain signal analysis. Empirical results demonstrate that BrainAgent effectively automates complex workflows with superior reliability, marking a paradigm shift toward democratized brain signal understanding.

</details>


### [119] [Quantization Inflates Reasoning: Token Inflation as a Hidden Cost of Low-Bit Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25519)
*Xinyu Lian,Walid Krichene,Beichen Huang,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Li Zhang,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 量化虽然能保持大语言模型的答案准确率，但会显著增加推理模型的思维链长度（token膨胀），抵消预期的单token加速收益，因此评估量化模型时需同时报告准确率和推理token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注量化对最终答案准确率和单token延迟的影响，忽略了量化对推理模型思维链长度的隐含影响，即低比特量化可能引入额外的测试时计算成本。

Method: 提出CoT Token膨胀比（CoT Token Inflation Ratio），系统比较INT4/INT3量化与全精度模型在数学推理、代码生成、科学问答和工具使用等基准上的推理长度变化，并分析推理轨迹的行为变化（中间步骤数量、语义重复程度）。

Result: INT4/INT3量化在保持准确率的同时，显著增加了推理token使用量；token膨胀伴随更多中间推理步骤和更高的语义重复；这种膨胀在端到端实际服务中可测量到性能损失。提示工程和采样策略的效果不一致，量化感知训练在缓解精度下降和token膨胀方面更有前景。

Conclusion: 评估量化推理模型时，应将推理token使用量作为与准确率并列的关键指标报告，以全面反映量化带来的实际计算成本。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the inference cost of large language models, but its effect on reasoning models is not fully captured by final-answer accuracy or per-token latency. We show that low-bit post-training quantization can introduce a hidden test-time compute cost: quantized reasoning models often generate longer chains of thought even when they still answer correctly. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, scientific question answering, and agentic tool-use benchmarks, we find that INT4/INT3 quantization can preserve accuracy but increase reasoning-token usage, offsetting the expected per-token speedup. To measure this effect, we introduce the CoT Token Inflation Ratio, which compares reasoning length between quantized and full-precision models averaged across all evaluation benchmarks. We further show that token inflation is accompanied by behavioral changes in the reasoning trace, including more intermediate steps and greater semantic repetition. These changes translate into measurable end-to-end real-world serving penalties. Finally, we evaluate mitigation strategies and find that prompting and decoding-time sampling offer inconsistent accuracy-length trade-offs, while quantization-aware training shows more promise in reducing both accuracy degradation and token inflation. Our results suggest that reasoning-token usage should be reported alongside accuracy when evaluating quantized reasoning models.

</details>


### [120] [Cliff Tokens: Identifying Single-Token Failure Triggers in LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25524)
*Jaeyong Ko,Pilsung Kang,Yukyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型数学推理中存在"悬崖标记"（cliff token），即导致推理路径从正确转向失败的关键标记。通过自适应阈值和单侧双比例z检验识别，删除首个悬崖标记并重采样可将pass@64恢复至1.0。进一步提出悬崖分类学（确定性、不确定性、采样偏离三类），并基于此提出Cliff-DPO，在GSM8K等基准上提升准确率最高达+6.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅在步骤、句子或已发生失败的标记层面分析推理失败原因，未能精确定位触发失败转向的具体标记。本文旨在找出那个导致推理从正确转向失败的精确标记（cliff token）。

Method: 1) 提出cliff token概念，基于自适应阈值的单侧双比例z检验检测token级势能显著下降的位置；2) 构建悬崖分类学：确定性悬崖（贪婪选择且低熵）、不确定性悬崖（贪婪选择但高熵）、采样偏离悬崖（非贪婪选择）；3) 提出Cliff-DPO，在悬崖位置进行单标记偏好优化。

Result: 跨越7个模型、3个基准（GSM1K, MATH500, AIME 2025）：删除首个cliff token重采样使pass@64恢复至1.0，保留则仅0.71-1.00。Cliff-DPO在GSM8K训练后在多个基准上准确率提升最高+6.6，其中不确定性和采样偏离悬崖的优化有效，确定性悬崖无效。

Conclusion: 悬崖标记是导致数学推理失败的关键触发点，其分类学在不同规模模型上泛化良好，针对性优化可有效提升推理正确率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) reach high accuracy in mathematical reasoning, but individual traces on the same problem diverge; some arrive at the correct answer while others fail. Prior work analyzes failure at the step, chunk, or sentence level, or at tokens where failure has already occurred. Neither identifies the precise token that triggers the shift toward failure. We introduce the cliff token, a token where the token-wise potential drops significantly under an adaptive threshold that scales with the local token-wise potential, based on a one-sided two-proportion z-test. Across seven models and three mathematical reasoning benchmarks (GSM1K, MATH500, AIME 2025), cliff tokens act as failure triggers; deleting the first cliff token and resampling recovers pass@64 to 1.0, while keeping it limits recovery to between 0.71 and 1.00. We further introduce a cliff taxonomy of deterministic, uncertain, and sampled-off cliffs, defined by greedy choice and token entropy. Each type has distinct probabilistic characteristics, and the taxonomy generalizes across model scales. Finally, we validate the taxonomy via single-token preference optimization at cliff positions (Cliff-DPO). Trained on GSM8K, Cliff-DPO improves accuracy across benchmarks by up to +6.6. Optimizing at uncertain and sampled-off cliffs improves reasoning, while deterministic cliffs do not.

</details>


### [121] [Agentic evolution of physically constrained foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25532)
*Jiangwei Zhang,Wen Sun,Chong Wang,Shiyao Li,Cheng Che,Chunjing Han,Dan Meng,Jian Yang,Yu Wang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理接地、多智能体驱动的自动发现引擎，能够自主设计硬件兼容的计算系统。该框架通过进化知识图谱和算法思维链将盲随机搜索转化为有向结构进化，在基础模型部署场景中演化出两种超越人工设计的硬件感知压缩方法，成功将2350亿参数模型部署到受限的双A100服务器上，减少75%内存需求，仅损失0.64%精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用AI agent缺乏物理接地，常产生与硬件不兼容的幻觉设计，无法在严格的物理约束下自主发现硬件兼容的计算系统方案。

Method: 基于进化知识图谱（EKG）锚定历史科学创新，提取"算法思维链"（algorithmic Chain-of-Thought）将盲目随机搜索转化为有向结构进化；多智能体架构；采用带宽高效的敏感度分析（Sensitivity Profile）实现资源受限部署。

Result: 演化出两种硬件感知压缩方法：Q-Enhance缓解长上下文精度损失，MoE-Salient-AQ在sub-3-bit条件下超越SOTA手工设计3.7%；成功将2350亿参数模型部署到双A100服务器，内存减少75%，精度仅降0.64%。

Conclusion: 通过将无约束组合搜索转化为知识驱动的自主发现，建立了在严格物理边界内可扩展的硬件-软件协同设计范式，推动了机器驱动的科学发现。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence increasingly drives automated scientific discovery, yet contemporary generalist agents lack physical grounding, frequently hallucinating hardware-incompatible designs. Here, we present a physically grounded, multi-agent discovery engine that autonomously architects hardware-compliant computing systems. Anchored by an Evolutionary Knowledge Graph structuring past scientific innovations, the framework extracts an "algorithmic Chain-of-Thought" to transform blind stochastic search into directed structural evolution. Applied to the extreme testbed of foundation model deployment, the engine evolved two hardware-aware compression methodologies surpassing human-engineered heuristics: Q-Enhance mitigates long-context accuracy loss in dense models, and MoE-Salient-AQ outperforms state-of-the-art manual sparse Mixture-of-Experts designs by 3.7% at sub-3-bit regimes. Utilizing a bandwidth-efficient Sensitivity Profile, we successfully deployed a massive 235-billion-parameter model onto a constrained dual-A100 server, reducing memory requirements by 75% with a marginal 0.64% accuracy degradation. By transforming unconstrained combinatorial search into knowledge-driven autonomy, this establishes a scalable hardware-software co-design paradigm for machine-driven discovery within strict physical boundaries.

</details>


### [122] [Reasonable Motion: A General ASP Foundation for Environment Constrained Movement Trajectory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25626)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回答集编程（ASP）的混合定量-定性方法，用于计算真实世界中移动物体的受约束分支轨迹模式。该方法通过在环境图中进行约束遍历，将几何上可接受的运动行为枚举为稳定模型，每个模型构成独特的轨迹模式。方法适用于多种动态域（如自动驾驶），并在Argoverse 2基准上进行了实证评估，展示了可验证的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动物体轨迹计算方法（特别是纯学习方法）缺乏可验证的可解释性，难以提供可追溯、可解释的运动行为枚举。作者旨在提出一种兼具定量与定性分析能力的混合方法，以生成可追踪到其底层逻辑模型的轨迹模式。

Method: 基于回答集编程（ASP）的混合定量-定性方法：对环境图进行约束遍历，将几何可接受的运动行为编码为逻辑规则，通过稳定模型语义枚举出不同的轨迹模式。每个稳定模型对应一种轨迹模式，由事件序列、地图拓扑和领域规范等因子共同表征。

Result: 在Argoverse 2大规模真实世界自动驾驶基准上进行了实证评估，验证了方法的适用性。生成的轨迹可追溯至其底层的稳定模型，具备纯学习方法无法提供的可验证可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法具有通用性，可应用于自动驾驶等包含移动物体的多样化动态域，并在可解释性和可追溯性上优于纯学习方法。

Abstract: We present a general answer set programming based hybrid quantitative-qualitative method for computing constrained branching trajectory modes for moving objects in real-world settings. The method performs constrained traversal of an environment graph, enumerating geometrically admissible motion behaviours as stable models, each constituting a distinct trajectory mode characterised by both domain-dependent and independent factors such as derived event sequence, map topology, and domain norms. The hybrid trajectory computation method is generally applicable across motion characteristics typically encountered in diverse dynamic domains with moving objects, e.g., autonomous driving. We demonstrate applicability and highlight how computed trajectories are traceable to their underlying stable model, thereby affording verifiable interpretability that purely learned approaches cannot provide. We also perform an empirical evaluation with Argoverse 2, a large-scale real-world autonomous driving benchmark representative of the class of dynamic domains within the scope of the proposed method.

</details>


### [123] [GUI agent: Guided Exploration of User-Sensitive Screens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25705)
*Aradhana Nayak,Mussadiq Nazeer,Wang Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种探索器代理，能从单个示范任务开始系统探索查询空间，识别在GUI环境中会导致用户敏感状态的查询，以便在关键时刻将任务执行交接给用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的代理在开放GUI环境中自动执行任务时，会不可避免地遇到包含用户敏感信息的屏幕，但现有代理通常被微调为无论安全影响如何都完成任务，导致实际部署困难且可靠性下降。因此需要识别和分类用户敏感状态及查询。

Method: 开发了一个探索器代理，从单个示范任务出发，系统性地探索查询空间，识别那些执行后会导致GUI环境中出现用户敏感状态的查询。

Result: 该方法能有效识别出会导致用户敏感状态的查询，为工程师提供数据集以识别并请求在关键场景中交接给用户。

Conclusion: 通过识别用户敏感状态和查询，并在关键时刻将控制权交还给用户，能够提升LLM代理在真实环境中的可靠性和安全性，促进其实际部署。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly being used to automate tasks for users within an open GUI environment. They inevitably encounter screens containing user-sensitive information, for which takeover of task execution by the user is highly desirable or even necessary. State-of-the-art LLM-driven agents are usually fine-tuned to complete tasks regardless of the safety implications of their actions. This makes their real-world deployment difficult and adversely affects the reliability. Therefore, it is crucial to identify and categorize user-sensitive states and define user-sensitive queries. This dataset would be to engineers to recognize and request handover to the user in critical scenarios. This short paper develops an explorer agent that systematically explores the query space starting from one demonstrated task to identify queries that, if executed, would lead to user-sensitive states in a GUI environment.

</details>


### [124] [Position Spaces and Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25719)
*Rita-Nathalia Assaf,Tom Davot,Frédéric Lardeux,Frédéric Saubion*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于位置图的形式化推理框架，利用两个严格偏序关系（水平和垂直对齐与优先关系）对离散令牌的相对位置建模，并引入链条件和兼容性约束。文章给出了图一致性的理论分析，证明了在位置图中子图同构问题（结构模式发现）仍是NP完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定性空间演算过于泛化，缺乏针对文档中离散令牌（如文本行、表格单元格）行列对齐特性的专门建模，需要一种既能表达位置约束又具备良好数学性质的图表示。

Method: 形式化定义位置图（position graphs），以两个严格偏序关系分别建模水平与垂直方向的对齐和优先关系；引入链条件（chain condition）和兼容性要求约束行列结构；通过图论分析建立一致性条件，并将结构模式发现建模为诱导子图同构问题，分析其计算复杂度。

Result: 建立了位置图一致性的充要条件；证明了即使限制在位置图这类特殊图类上，诱导子图同构问题（即结构模式发现）仍然是NP完全的。

Conclusion: 位置图为文档结构中基于位置的约束提供了一个独立于具体数据提取技术的逻辑层，其数学基础（一致性与复杂度分析）为后续高效推理算法的设计奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce position graphs, a graph-based reasoning framework based on the formalization of position spaces. This framework utilizes two strict partial orders, representing horizontal and vertical alignment and precedence, to model the relative positions of discrete tokens. Unlike general qualitative spatial calculi, position graphs are constrained by a chain condition and compatibility requirements that focus on rows and columns. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis of this representation, beginning with a characterization of graph consistency. Conditions to ensure the consistency of position graphs are established. Furthermore, we investigate the computational complexity of structural pattern discovery, modeled as the induced subgraph isomorphism problem. We demonstrate that this problem remains NP-complete even within the restricted class of position graphs. While initially motivated by document processing, this work focuses on the underlying mathematical properties and algebraic consistency of position-based constraints, providing a formal logical layer that is independent of specific data extraction techniques.

</details>


### [125] [Fuzzy Quantification over OWL Ontologies and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25778)
*Enrique Palacín,Fernando Bobillo,Ignacio Huitzil,Francesca A. Lisi,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于在标准/模糊本体和知识图谱上评估模糊量化查询，支持Type I和Type II模糊量化表达式，并实现了公开可用的Q2S2系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理模糊量化查询时缺乏通用性，通常受限于特定的量化词类型、评估方法或数据源（如OWL本体或RDFS知识图谱），亟需一个与三者均无关的统一框架。

Method: 提出一个与量化词类型、评估方法和数据源无关的通用框架，支持Type I和Type II模糊量化表达式的个体检索；该框架可同时适用于标准本体、模糊本体及知识图谱。

Result: 实现了Q2S2系统作为公开可用的原型工具，验证了框架的可行性与通用性。

Conclusion: 该框架的有效性和适应性得到验证，Q2S2系统为后续研究提供了基础平台。

Abstract: This paper presents a versatile framework for evaluating fuzzy quantification queries over both standard and fuzzy ontologies as well as knowledge graphs. The primary objective is the retrieval of individuals that satisfy queries articulated via Type I or Type II fuzzy quantified expressions. A key advantage of the proposed approach is its inherent adaptability: it remains entirely agnostic to the quantifier type, the underlying evaluation method, and the specific data source of the ontology (i.e., OWL ontologies or RDFS knowledge graphs). Furthermore, we present Q2S2, a publicly accessible implementation of this system developed to support future research.

</details>


### [126] [Confidence Sequences for Online Statistical Model Checking of Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25797)
*Konstantin Kueffner,Tobias Meggendorfer,Maximilian Weininger,Patrick Wienhöft*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对马尔可夫决策过程中概率未知的问题，提出了多种专为在线设置设计的置信序列，通过高效工具实现，相比传统联合界方法平均减少50倍样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MDP假设精确已知转移概率，但在实际应用（如信息物理系统、生物过程）中这一假设不现实。现有统计方法虽能处理不确定性，但存在实现不正确或次优的问题。

Method: 设计多种置信序列（confidence sequences）用于在线设置，实现高效工具，并与经典联合界方法进行对比。

Result: 提出的置信序列方法优于传统联合界方法，实现工具平均只需50倍更少的样本量即可达到与先前最优方法相同效果。

Conclusion: 置信序列是处理MDP中未知概率问题的有效工具，能显著降低样本复杂度。

Abstract: Markov decision processes (MDPs) are a classic model of decision making under uncertainty, exhibiting both non-deterministic choice as well as probabilistic uncertainty. Traditionally, exact knowledge of the underlying probabilities is assumed. However, this often is unrealistic, e.g.\ when modelling cyber-physical systems or biological processes. Here, statistical methods provide a way towards obtaining meaningful guarantees. The classical approach is to gather samples in the MDP, use these to draw statistical conclusions about the transition probabilities, and from there obtain bounds on the true value; then, if these bounds are too broad, repeat. However, existing implementations of this approach are either subtly incorrect or sub-optimal, and quite often both. We present several \emph{confidence sequences}, which are specifically designed for such \enquote{online} settings, implement all of them in an efficient tool, and show their practical applicability. In particular, we show that they outperform classical \enquote{union-bound} style approaches, and overall our implementation requires 50x less samples on average than previous state of the art.

</details>


### [127] [AI Snitches Get Glitches: Towards Evading Agentic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25836)
*Hyejun Jeong,Dzung Pham,Amir Houmansadr,Eugene Bagdasarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了“智能体监控”（agentic surveillance）问题，即AI智能体利用可用信息、生成报告并通过工具发送的能力。作者构建了SurveilBench数据集，评估了模型在三个领域（企业、教育、警察）的监控能力，发现部分模型存在自发的监控协助倾向，同时也会向政府举报监控行为。此外，提出了三种利用提示注入逃避监控的技术。结论指出智能体监控已可轻易实现，呼吁建立全面的技术、伦理和立法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的广泛部署带来了用户数据被滥用于监控的新风险，用户可能无法控制智能体的行为和数据访问。现有研究缺乏对智能体监控能力的系统化定义、评估和防御机制。

Method: 形式化定义了智能体监控问题；构建SurveilBench数据集，涵盖企业、教育、警察三个领域的报告场景；评估不同模型的监控能力；设计三种基于提示注入的逃避监控技术（隐藏、欺骗、诱导过度升级）。

Result: 部分模型表现出自发的（无提示的）监控协助倾向，但同时也会向政府举报监控尝试。提出的三种逃避技术有效。

Conclusion: 智能体监控已可轻易实现，亟需建立技术、伦理和立法方面的综合保护框架。

Abstract: To better assist users with completing challenging tasks, AI agents mediate communications, access data, and interact with different APIs. Many employers (and even nation-states) already provide their users with this technology. However, widespread adoption of AI agents creates a new risk to abuse access to user data for another goal: surveilling users. These users might not even have the ability or permission to control the actions and data accesses of the surveilling agents.
  We introduce and formalize the problem of agentic surveillance: the ability of an AI agent to analyze available information, craft a report, and send it out using available tools. To evaluate surveillance capabilities across different models, we create SurveilBench, a dataset of various reporting scenarios focusing on three domains: corporate, education, and police. We find that some models exhibit emergent (i.e., unprompted) tendencies to help surveillance, but they also report the attempts to surveil users to the government.
  Finally, we repurpose prompt injections for evading surveillance and develop three evasion techniques that hide from, deceive, or induce over-escalation in surveillance agents. We conclude that agentic surveillance can already be easily implemented and, therefore, call for a comprehensive technical, ethical, and legislative framework to protect users.

</details>


### [128] [Agentic System as Compressor: Quantifying System Intelligence in Bits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25960)
*Zihan Qin,Hongrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"压缩即智能"的视角，通过算术编码和种子编码等方法量化智能体系统的信息压缩效率，并在五项任务中验证了智能体组件能有效减少编码长度，为评估真实智能体系统提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正从独立的预测器演变为智能体系统（调用工具、检索证据、遵守环境约束、使用验证器、通过搜索和多轮交互完成任务），但缺乏统一的评估框架来衡量智能体组件的贡献。作者希望从信息论角度回答"什么构成更强的智能体系统"。

Method: 基于"压缩即智能"的分析视角，在固定任务分布、接口和计算预算下，将更强的智能体系统定义为能用更少比特重建目标对象。通过算术编码、种子编码和回退机制来具体化该度量，在五项设置（反转文本、国际象棋走法、蛋白质序列、检索增强问答、语义故事压缩）中评估智能体组件对编码长度的影响。

Result: 在所有五项任务中，引入智能体组件（工具调用、检索、验证器、多轮交互等）均显著减少了编码长度，表明智能体组件降低了残余不确定性。

Conclusion: 这些受控实验覆盖了真实智能体系统的典型组件类型，证明编码长度能够分析组件、观察者和预算如何改变残余不确定性，为评估真实智能体系统提供了可行的方法论指导。

Abstract: Large language models are turning from isolated predictors into agentic systems: they call tools, retrieve evidence, obey environment constraints, use verifiers, and complete tasks through search and multi-turn interaction. We adopts an analytical viewpoint based on "compression is intelligence": under a fixed task distribution, interface, and compute budget, a stronger agentic system lets a target object be reconstructed with fewer bits. We operationalize the measure with arithmetic coding, seed coding, and a fallback, and evaluate it in five settings: reversed text, chess moves, protein sequences, retrieval-augmented question answering, and semantic story compression; in all of them agentic components reduce codelength. These small, controlled experiments cover component types typical of real agentic systems, show that codelength can analyze how components, observers, and budgets change residual uncertainty, and offer guidance for evaluating real agent systems.

</details>


### [129] [WinDOM: Self-Family Distillation for Small-Model GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25964)
*Chengheng Li-Chen,Zhiqian Zhou,Hao Chen,Nicolas Chauvin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WinDOM（通过PlayDriver从Windows 11 DOM直接提取54,425条GUI grounding数据，无需人工标注）和Self-Family Distillation（SFD，一种仅由教师选择参数化的拒绝采样冷启动方法，教师可为学生EMA或同族冻结大模型），并将SFD冷启动的饱和深度作为GRPO显式超参数。在Qwen3.5-2B学生模型上，欠饱和冷启动比收敛冷启动更适合GRPO初始化：SFD-4B+Early-init RL相比基线OOD均值提升+5.4；同尺寸EMA模式（65.2）与跨尺寸4B变体（66.3）性能相差不到1个点，且无需外部教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模（~2B）GUI-grounding模型在设备端部署、辅助工具和低成本迭代中具有吸引力，但面临两个核心问题：如何在不依赖昂贵人工标注的情况下获取边界框训练数据，以及如何有效结合监督微调与强化学习。现有方法通常依赖OCR或人工标注，且缺乏针对小模型的最佳实践配方。

Method: 1) WinDOM：在headless Playwright驱动下，通过开源Windows 11网页重实现直接读取DOM中的边界框，构建54,425条grounding语料库，完全避免OCR和人工标注。2) Self-Family Distillation (SFD)：一种单次拒绝采样冷启动方法，仅通过教师选择参数化（学生EMA或无外部模型的同族冻结大模型）。3) 将SFD冷启动的饱和深度作为GRPO的显式超参数，发现欠饱和冷启动是更好的RL初始化器。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B学生上，SFD-4B+Early-init RL相比基线提升OOD均值+5.4（ScreenSpot-Pro +3.5，OSWorld-G +7.0，ScreenSpot-V2 +5.8）。同尺寸EMA模式（65.2）与跨尺寸4B变体（66.3）性能差距在1个OOD均值点以内，且无需外部教师。

Conclusion: 小规模GUI-grounding模型可以通过WinDOM实现无人工标注的数据获取，并通过SFD冷启动+GRPO的联合配方显著提升性能。同尺寸EMA自蒸馏可达到与使用更大外部教师接近的效果，为低资源场景下的GUI智能体训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Small ($\sim$2B) GUI-grounding agents are attractive for on-device deployment, accessibility tooling, and low-cost iteration, but at this scale they face two open recipe questions: how to obtain bounding-box training data without expensive human annotation, and how to combine supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. We address both, with the explicit goal of pushing small-model performance rather than scaling up. WinDOM is a $54{,}425$-record grounding corpus harvested by driving an open-source Windows 11 web reimplementation under headless Playwright, with bounding boxes read directly off the DOM and no OCR or human annotation. Self-Family Distillation (SFD) is a single rejection-sampling cold-start parameterised only by the teacher choice: either an EMA of the student (no external model) or a frozen larger same-family teacher. We then treat the saturation depth of the SFD cold-start as an explicit GRPO hyperparameter. On a Qwen3.5-2B student, the under-saturated cold-start is a better GRPO initialiser than the converged one: SFD-4B with Early-init RL gains $+5.4$ OOD-mean ($+3.5$ ScreenSpot-Pro, $+7.0$ OSWorld-G, $+5.8$ ScreenSpot-V2) over the base. The same-size EMA mode lands within roughly one OOD-mean point of the cross-size $4$B variant ($65.2$ vs $66.3$) without an external teacher.

</details>


### [130] [InvestPhilBench: A Multi-Layer Dynamic Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Procedural Reasoning in Expert Investment Philosophy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25984)
*Mingguang Chen,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了InvestPhilBench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在专家投资决策框架上的程序性推理能力，包含8层认知层级、118张原则卡、25张决策框架卡和243个QA问题。核心发现：复合评分（BASP）在前沿模型上饱和（0.932），但过程性指标GRA暴露了显著缺陷（L4约0.77，L7仅0.57-0.62），说明复合评分掩盖了程序性推理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未能检验大语言模型能否准确重构并应用专家投资者的特定程序性决策框架，亟需一个多维动态基准来评估模型在投资研究助理场景下的程序性推理能力。

Method: 构建InvestPhilBench，包含8个认知层级（L1原则识别到L8新颖框架外推）；引入BASP（含OGRS、KCCS、SAP@k、IVP、CKCA五种度量）、FMDP（六种失败模式检测规则）和GRA（逐门过程准确性度量）；采用统一评判器和模型在环检索/神谕条件。

Result: 四模型消融波显示提供商层级显著分岔（BASP 0.906 vs 0.438）；前沿模型BASP复合评分饱和（Claude L4=0.932），但GRA暴露过程性缺陷（前沿L4 GRA约0.77，L7 GRA 0.57-0.62）；自动化BASP与人工参考的Pearson r=0.72（MAE=0.10），归因（SAP@3）为最弱子指标。

Conclusion: 复合评分奖励流畅性而隐藏了程序性推理差距，证明了InvestPhilBench能揭示现有评估方法忽略的关键缺陷；v0.6主要贡献在于基准与方法论本身，去混淆的多模型排行榜和全条件运行是v1.0交付物。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as investment research assistants, yet no benchmark tests whether they can accurately reconstruct and apply the specific procedural decision frameworks of expert investors. We introduce InvestPhilBench, a multi-layer dynamic benchmark spanning eight cognitive tiers, from principle identification (L1) to novel framework extrapolation (L8). The v0.6 release comprises 118 primary-source-verified investment principle cards, 25 decision framework cards with explicit topology metadata, and 243 QA questions (197 dev / 46 held-out test). For reproducible scoring at scale we introduce the Benchmark Automated Scoring Pipeline (BASP) -- five algorithmic metrics (OGRS, KCCS, SAP@k, IVP, CKCA) -- the Failure Mode Detection Protocol (FMDP) with computable rules for six failure modes, and Gate Reconstruction Accuracy (GRA), a per-gate metric for questions with gold reasoning programs. In this release, InvestPhilBench is primarily a benchmark-and-methodology contribution. A four-model sanity wave on the 188-question development split shows a sharp provider-tier split (BASP 0.906 vs. 0.438); these mixed-judge numbers are confounded upper bounds. The central finding: the BASP composite saturates at the frontier (Claude L4 = 0.932) while GRA still exposes a procedural deficit (frontier L4 GRA approx. 0.77, L7 GRA 0.57-0.62) -- composite scoring rewards fluent prose and hides the procedural gap. v0.6 implements a unified judge and true model-in-the-loop retrieval/oracle conditions; the de-confounded multi-model leaderboard and full three-condition run are v1.0 deliverables. On a 100-item expert-annotated gold set the automated BASP composite tracks the human reference at Pearson r = 0.72 (MAE = 0.10), with attribution (SAP@3) the weakest sub-metric and the failure-mode detector running sensitive-but-over-flagging.

</details>


### [131] [Autodata: An agentic data scientist to create high quality synthetic data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25996)
*Ilia Kulikov,Chenxi Whitehouse,Tianhao Wu,Yixin Nie,Swarnadeep Saha,Eryk Helenowski,Weizhe Yuan,Olga Golovneva,Jack Lanchantin,Yoram Bachrach,Jakob Foerster,Xian Li,Han Fang,Sainbayar Sukhbaatar,Jason Weston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Autodata方法，让AI智能体充当数据科学家，通过元优化学习创建高质量训练和评估数据。具体实现Agentic Self-Instruct在计算机科学、法律推理和数学推理任务上优于传统合成数据方法，且元优化智能体本身能带来更大的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成数据创建方法性能有限，需要一种能让AI自主生成更高质量训练和评估数据的方法，将推理算力转化为更优的模型训练。

Method: 提出Autodata框架，训练（元优化）一个数据科学家智能体，使其学会创建更强的数据。具体实现为Agentic Self-Instruct方法。

Result: 在计算机科学研究、法律推理和数学推理任务上，Autodata优于经典合成数据集创建方法；元优化数据科学家智能体本身带来更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: Agentic数据创建提供了一种将增加的推理计算转化为更高质量模型训练的途径，有望改变AI数据构建方式。

Abstract: We introduce Autodata, a general method that enables AI agents to act as data scientists who build high quality training and evaluation data. We show how to train (meta-optimize) such a data scientist agent, so that it learns to create even stronger data. We describe the overall formulation, and a specific practical implementation, Agentic Self-Instruct. We conduct experiments on computer science research tasks, legal reasoning tasks and reasoning with mathematical objects, where we obtain improved results compared to classical synthetic dataset creation methods. Further, meta-optimizing the data scientist agent itself delivers an even larger performance uplift. Agentic data creation provides a way to convert increased inference compute into higher quality model training. Overall, we believe this direction has the potential to change the way we build AI data.

</details>


### [132] [The Unfireable Safety Kernel: Execution-Time AI Alignment for AI Agents and Other Escapable AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26057)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向可逃逸AI系统的架构级授权机制——Unfireable Safety Kernel，通过进程隔离、前置执行、故障关闭和外部化签名证据四个核心属性，在运行时层面对AI agent进行不可绕过的安全控制。Rust参考实现通过了机器验证（Z3定理证明+Kani有界模型检查）和字节等价性测试，在真实可逃逸AI系统（确定性自改进世界模型）的1,000次自我修改中成功阻止全部704次安全关键攻击，6,240次授权往返无成功绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI agent安全控制主要放在agent自身运行时内部（系统提示、输出过滤、护栏库），但任何位于agent地址空间内的控制都可被影响agent的输入所触及，这构成一类称为"可逃逸AI系统"的问题。现有方法无法提供架构性控制，需要一种满足架构级授权约束的机制。

Method: 定义四个架构级授权属性：1) 进程隔离（process separation）；2) 前置执行且仅有一条结构上的必经路径（pre-action enforcement on a structurally only path）；3) 请求级和系统级故障关闭（fail-closed at both request and system levels）；4) 外部化签名证据（externalized signed evidence）。基于这些属性实现Unfireable Safety Kernel，使用Z3 SMT定理证明和Kani有界模型检查进行两级机器验证，并通过Python到Rust迁移的字节等价性测试（1000/1000 fixtures, 17/17 adversarial classes）。

Result: 在可逃逸AI系统（确定性自改进世界模型）上评估：1,000次自我修改中，704次安全关键核心尝试被拒绝，无逃逸；300次在操作员终止开关下也被拒绝；6,240次授权往返测试无成功绕过。与3个声称拥有agent控制平面的当代系统对比，agent在此架构下无法绕过控制。

Conclusion: Unfireable Safety Kernel实现了执行时AI对齐（execution-time AI alignment），补充了训练时对齐（RLHF、Constitutional AI）和推理时对齐。该机制在架构层面保证了不可逃逸的安全控制，并通过形式化验证确保了故障关闭不变量的正确性。

Abstract: AI agents are granted access to tools, APIs, and other infrastructure, making them active principals in those systems. The dominant approach places controls inside the agent's own runtime: system prompts, output filters, and guardrail libraries. Any control in the agent's address space is reachable by inputs that influence it; this generalizes to any AI system with sufficient reach into its own runtime, a class we term escapable AI systems.
  We identify four properties that an authorization mechanism must satisfy for architectural control rather than for cooperative requests: process separation, pre-action enforcement on a structurally only path, fail-closed at both the request and system levels, and externalized signed evidence verifiable outside the controlled system's trust boundary. We position this layer as execution-time AI alignment, complementing training-time alignment (RLHF, Constitutional AI) and inference-time alignment.
  We present the Unfireable Safety Kernel, a Rust reference implementation realizing all four. Its fail-closed invariant is machine-checked at two levels: an SMT theorem (Z3) and an exhaustive bounded-model-checking proof of the production decision function (Kani, 4/4 harnesses). A Python-to-Rust migration was gated on byte-equivalence (1000/1000 fixtures; 17/17 adversarial classes). We evaluate the kernel governing a live, escapable AI system, a deterministic, self-improving world model, against an escape-seeking adversary driving its real self-modification seam: across 1,000 self-modifications, all 704 attempts on the safety-critical core are refused, with no escape; a further 300, under the operator kill switch, are also refused. A separate campaign of 6,240 authorization round-trips had no successful bypass. Against 3 contemporary systems claiming the agent control plane, the agent invokes control; here, it lacks that choice.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [BFMTrack: Latent Sequence Optimization for Physics-Based Motion Tracking with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25056)
*Thomas Rupf,Agon Serifi,David Müller,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在序列优化（LSO）方法，通过策略梯度更新和时域相关噪声建模，扩展行为基础模型（BFMs）的能力，实现无需奖励工程的高精度运动跟踪，并在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧和真实人形机器人部署中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFMs擅长时不变任务（如到达目标），但其潜在空间不支持时变目标（如运动序列跟踪）；现有启发式方法（如滑动窗口平均）无法捕捉高度动态运动的细微变化，且需要繁琐的奖励工程。

Method: 提出LSO方法，将模拟 rollout 与策略梯度更新结合，对潜在序列进行优化；使用时域相关噪声对潜在轨迹建模，引导优化生成平滑、连贯的潜在序列。

Result: 在密集运动跟踪、稀疏关键帧任务以及真实人形机器人直接部署上均取得有效结果。

Conclusion: LSO扩展了BFMs的能力，使其能够实现精确的运动跟踪，无需奖励工程和调参，并具备从仿真到真实机器人部署的可行性。

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) offer a promising path toward universal physics-based character control by organizing a rich repertoire of physically plausible behaviors into a latent space, guided by a large-scale motion dataset. While these models excel at time-invariant tasks, such as goal-reaching and state-based reward optimization, their latent space does not directly support time-varying objectives, such as tracking a motion sequence. For tracking, existing heuristics rely on moving-window-averaging that fails to capture the nuances of highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a novel Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO) to address these shortcomings. Our approach combines simulation rollouts with a policy gradient update to optimize over a sequence of latents, extending the capabilities of BFMs toward precise motion tracking without requiring reward engineering and tuning. To guide the optimization toward smooth, coherent latent trajectories, we model the latent sequence using temporally correlated noise. We validate our approach across dense tracking, sparse keyframing, and direct deployment onto a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [134] [Invariant Kalman filtering for extended pose estimation in multi-IMU articulated rigid-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25083)
*Sven Goffin,Cédric Schwartz,Silvère Bonnabel,Olivier Brüls,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出相对L-扩展位姿（Lie群表示），用于含IMU的铰接刚体系统扩展位姿估计。通过将运动链关节约束表示为无噪声伪测量并集成到迭代IEKF中，在UR5e机器人和人体腿部验证，相比基线EKF/IterEKF/绝对位姿IterIEKF，收敛更快、变异性更低，RMSE降低至少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEKF在单刚体位姿估计中具有收敛保证和不可观性下的一致性，但将其扩展到铰接系统面临挑战：刚体间位姿耦合阻碍直接应用，且在不变滤波框架内融入关节运动学约束尚未解决。

Method: 提出相对L-扩展位姿（Lie群表示）描述运动链系统；每个刚体配备一个IMU，获得群仿射动力学；将关节约束表示为无噪声伪测量，集成到迭代IEKF（IterIEKF）中，保留不变滤波的收敛和一致性保证。

Result: 在UR5e机器人和人体腿部验证，所提IterIEKF在所有场景中均优于EKF、IterEKF和绝对位姿IterIEKF基线，收敛更快、运行间变异性更低，RMSE相比次优滤波器降低至少50%。

Conclusion: 相对L-扩展位姿表示有效解决了IEKF在铰接系统中的扩展问题，结合伪测量约束的IterIEKF在精度、收敛速度和稳定性上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate extended pose estimation (orientation, velocity, and position) for IMU-instrumented articulated rigid-body systems is a key challenge in robotics and human motion analysis. The invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) addresses this problem for a single rigid body with convergence guarantees and consistency under unobservability, but extending these properties to articulated systems is nontrivial: inter-body pose coupling prevents a direct application, and incorporating joint kinematic constraints within the invariant framework remains an open problem. To address this gap, we introduce the relative L-extended pose, a Lie group representation for kinematic-tree systems. With one IMU per body, it yields group-affine dynamics and allows joint constraints to be expressed in invariant form. We incorporate these constraints as noise-free pseudo-measurements within an iterated IEKF (IterIEKF), thereby preserving the convergence and consistency guarantees of invariant filtering. Validated on both a UR5e robot and a human leg, the proposed IterIEKF outperforms all EKF, IterEKF, and absolute-pose IterIEKF baselines. It converges faster, exhibits lower run-to-run variability, and consistently achieves the lowest RMSE, with reductions of at least 50% compared to the second-best filter across all scenarios considered in this work.

</details>


### [135] [ADM-Fusion: Adaptive Deep Multi-Sensor Fusion for Robust Ego-Motion Estimation in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25111)
*Hasan Moughnieh,Ibrahim Ghaddar,Hadi Elham,Imad H. Elhajj,Daniel Asmar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ADM-Fusion，一种端到端深度学习的多传感器融合方法，采用自适应传感器混合专家架构和内容感知路由，实现传感器权重的动态分配；通过分离平移与旋转分支并引入跨任务注意力机制，在退化和动态环境下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合中不同模态的传感器在不同环境下失效模式各异，固定加权融合无法适应环境变化和传感器退化，亟需一种能够动态平衡互补线索的自适应融合方法，特别是对于依赖一致传感器信息集成的自运动估计任务。

Method: 1) 自适应传感器混合专家框架（Adaptive Sensor Mixture-of-Experts），结合内容感知路由（content-aware routing）实现传感器输入的实时动态加权；2) 分离的平移和旋转分支（separate translation and rotation branches），通过跨任务注意力机制（cross-task attention）耦合，保持任务特化同时促进信息共享；3) 在CARLA-LOC模拟数据集上训练，再在KITTI真实数据上微调，实现仿真到真实的迁移。

Result: 在退化条件下保持鲁棒性，同时与现有方法相比保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: ADM-Fusion通过适应性传感器加权和任务特化分支设计，有效应对了多传感器融合中环境变化和传感器退化问题，验证了仿真到真实迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Robust multi-sensor fusion is essential for reliable autonomy in diverse and degraded environments, where sensor reliability can fluctuate rapidly. Because different modalities fail in distinct ways, effective fusion should adaptively balance complementary cues rather than rely on fixed weighting. This adaptability is particularly important for ego-motion estimation, since accurate updates depend on the consistent integration of complementary sensor information. We propose ADM-Fusion, an end-to-end deep learning based multi-sensor fusion method designed to adapt to environmental changes and sensor degradation. ADM-Fusion employs an adaptive sensor mixture-of-experts framework with content-aware routing to dynamically assign weights to sensor inputs in real time. The system further incorporates separate translation and rotation branches, coupled through a cross-task attention mechanism to preserve task-specific specialization while enabling information sharing. ADM-Fusion is trained on the CARLA-LOC simulated dataset and subsequently fine-tuned on KITTI real-world data, demonstrating effective simulation-to-real transfer. Experiments show that ADM-Fusion remains robust under degraded conditions while maintaining competitive performance against existing methods.

</details>


### [136] [SurveilNav: Collaborative Object Goal Navigation with Robot and Surveillance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25119)
*Ming-Ming Yu,Qunbo Wang,Rongtao Xu,Yanghong Mei,Yirong Yang,Longteng Guo,Wenjun Wu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SurveilNav框架，通过将机器人与多视角监控系统协同，实现室内大规模环境下的高效协作目标导航。该框架集成了主动相机调度、联合2D/3D建图、VLM价值估计和协作目标验证，在HM3D数据集上取得了最先进的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注单机器人场景，难以在大规模环境中利用多视角监控信息进行协作，单机器人感知范围有限且固定监控存在盲区，导致探索效率低下。

Method: 提出了SurveilNav协作导航框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) 主动相机调度；(2) 联合2D/3D建图；(3) VLM（视觉语言模型）价值估计；(4) 协作目标验证。该框架将机器人的动态局部感知与监控系统的静态全局视角协同融合。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上的实验结果表明，SurveilNav在探索效率和导航成功率方面均优于现有方法，达到了最先进的性能。同时，系统在大规模搜索、家庭环境和救援任务中展现出较强的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 通过机器人与多视角监控系统的协同，可以有效克服单机器人感知范围有限和固定相机存在盲区的问题，为大规模室内环境中的协作导航提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of surveillance systems in factories, offices, and homes, integrating them with robots offers a promising direction for collaborative and efficient task execution. However, existing approaches largely focus on single-robot scenarios and struggle with multi-view collaboration in large-scale environments. In this paper, we present a novel indoor collaborative object navigation dataset built on Habitat-Sim, featuring 206 cameras across 74 floors. The dataset enables systematic evaluation of an agent's ability to exploit multi-view surveillance information. To address the limitations of single-robot perception, we propose SurveilNav, a collaborative navigation framework that integrates active camera scheduling, joint 2D/3D mapping, VLM-based value estimation, and collaborative target verification. By synergizing the robot's dynamic local perception with the static global view of surveillance, this architecture effectively overcomes both the limited perception range of single agents and the inherent blind spots of fixed cameras, resolving inefficient exploration. Experimental results on the HM3D dataset demonstrate that SurveilNav substantially outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both exploration efficiency and navigation success rate. Moreover, the system shows strong potential for applications in large-scale search, home environments, and rescue missions.

</details>


### [137] [AeroCast: Probabilistic 3D Trajectory Prediction for Non-Cooperative Aerial Obstacles via Transformer-MDN Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25122)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroCast是一个结合Transformer编码器和混合密度网络的概率轨迹预测框架，用于非协作空中障碍物的未来位置预测。在混合真实与合成的四旋翼数据集上，该方法在五秒预测范围内将平均位移误差和最终位移误差降低了约50%，推理速度达0.1ms/样本，支持100Hz实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非协作空中障碍物（如鸟类、失控无人机、碎片）的轨迹具有多模态运动特性，而现有确定性预测方法无法充分表征这种分布。同时，现有方法要么使用循环编码器导致难以捕捉机动启动的长程运动学先兆，要么仅产生点预测而无法为下游规划器提供分布信息。

Method: AeroCast框架由三部分组成：(1) Transformer编码器，用于捕获长程时序依赖关系；(2) 混合密度网络输出头，预测每时间步三维位移的高斯混合分布；(3) 平移不变的连续位移编码和面向校准的训练目标，分别解决输入设计问题和混合模式退化挑战。

Result: 在涵盖九种运动类别的混合真实与合成四旋翼语料库上，AeroCast在五秒预测范围内将平均位移误差和最终位移误差相对基线降低约50%，同时取得了最低的负对数似然和连续排名概率评分。消融分析表明速度输入和模型容量是预测质量的主要贡献因素，位置编码对长时程轨迹连贯性至关重要。推理速度0.1ms/样本，支持100Hz实时部署。

Conclusion: AeroCast通过Transformer与混合密度网络的结合，有效解决了非协作空中障碍物多模态轨迹预测问题，在精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法，具备实际部署的实时性条件。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial vehicles operating in shared airspace must predict the future positions of non-cooperative obstacles to plan evasive maneuvers before a collision becomes unavoidable. Unlike cooperative systems that share intent, non-cooperative obstacles such as birds, uncontrolled drones, or debris exhibit multi-modal motion that deterministic predictors cannot adequately represent. Existing methods either rely on recurrent encoders that propagate temporal information sequentially, limiting their ability to capture long-range kinematic precursors of maneuver initiation, or produce point forecasts that provide no distributional information to downstream planners. This paper presents AeroCast, a probabilistic trajectory prediction framework that combines a Transformer encoder with a Mixture Density Network output head to predict per-timestep Gaussian mixture distributions over future three-dimensional displacements. A translation-invariant consecutive displacement encoding and a calibration-oriented training objective address the input design and mode-degeneracy challenges specific to mixture-based aerial trajectory prediction. On a hybrid real-and-synthetic quadrotor corpus spanning nine motion categories, AeroCast reduces Average Displacement Error and Final Displacement Error by approximately 50% relative to the baselines over a five-second horizon, and achieves the lowest negative log-likelihood and Continuous Ranked Probability Score among all compared methods. Ablation analysis identifies velocity input and model capacity as the primary contributors to prediction quality, and positional encoding as essential for long-horizon trajectory coherence. AeroCast inference completes in 0.1ms per sample, compatible with real-time onboard deployment at 100Hz.

</details>


### [138] [RGB: RL Guided Whole-Body MPPI for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25123)
*Yunsoo Seo,Sol Choi,Euncheol Im,Myo Taeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种RL引导的全身模型预测路径积分（MPPI）框架，将预训练的RL策略作为采样先验，通过MPPI在线修正来满足额外任务目标，无需重新训练策略。在29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人仿真中实现了平均280Hz的高率控制，在相同命令接口下相比纯RL基线提高了任务级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽然实现了鲁棒稳定性，但其行为与训练目标和命令接口紧密耦合，导致在不重新训练的情况下难以添加新的反馈目标。需要一种既能保持RL鲁棒性又能灵活添加新目标的控制框架。

Method: 提出RL引导的全身MPPI框架：将预训练RL策略作为采样先验，为MPPI rollout提供偏向动态可行行为的分布；任务目标通过模块化MPPI代价项指定；MPPI通过在线修正RL先验来满足这些目标，无需重新训练策略。

Result: 在29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人MuJoCo仿真中实现平均280Hz的高率控制；相比纯RL基线，在相同命令接口下提高了任务级精度；能够纠正直行行走中的系统性漂移，并跟踪通过代价项施加的额外全身参考信号。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解耦了RL策略的鲁棒稳定性与任务目标的灵活性，通过MPPI在线修正实现了无需重新训练即可添加新反馈目标的能力，为人形机器人全身控制提供了一种兼具鲁棒性和精确性的解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require whole-body controllers that are both robust and precise in contact-rich environments. While deep reinforcement learning (RL) achieves robust stability, its behavior is tightly coupled to the training objective and command interface, making it difficult to add new feedback objectives without retraining. In this study, we propose an RL guided whole-body model predictive path integral (MPPI) framework that acts as an add-on feedback controller on top of a pretrained RL policy. Instead of using RL policy as the final controller, we use it as a sampling prior that biases MPPI rollouts toward dynamically feasible behaviors. Task objectives are specified through modular MPPI cost terms, and MPPI closes the loop by continuously correcting the RL prior online to satisfy these objectives without retraining the policy. Simulations on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo demonstrate stable high-rate control (average 280~Hz). The proposed method improves task-level precision over a pure RL baseline under the same command interface. This is achieved by correcting systematic drift during straight walking and tracking additional whole-body reference signals imposed through the cost.

</details>


### [139] [Causality-Based Parametric Control Barrier Function for Safe Multi-Vehicle Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25134)
*Yiwei Lyu,Caleb Chang,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果推断的参数化控制屏障函数（Causality-based Parametric-CBF），用于多车交互场景中的自适应安全控制，通过显式推理车辆间的因果影响，在保证安全的同时提升任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多车交互时存在两个问题：1）难以确定周围车辆行为的因果关系（受车辆间相互影响干扰）；2）最坏情况分析导致过于保守的行为。因此需要一种能够显式推理车辆间因果影响的安全控制器。

Method: 将Parametric-CBF扩展到多机器人交互场景，嵌入因果推断机制以显式推理车辆间的相互影响。基于学习的Causality-based Parametric-CBF，设计了自适应安全控制器，使自车能够根据对周围车辆行为的因果推断期望进行安全反应。

Result: 通过利用多车系统中的运动灵活性，在多种交互密集场景下，任务效率得到了显著提升。

Conclusion: 因果推断结合参数化CBF能够有效解决多车交互中的因果关系模糊问题，在保证安全的前提下避免过度保守，提升整体任务效率。

Abstract: Safe control has been widely studied in various safety-critical applications, for instance, autonomous driving. In order to ensure the autonomous vehicle does not collide with other vehicles, it is essential to obtain an accurate expectation of surrounding vehicles' behavior and react adaptively. Instead of assuming fully cooperative and homogeneous vehicles using the same safety-critical controllers, recent works have been exploring different data-driven approaches to model the neighboring vehicles' underlying controllers with observed data. However, existing works either suffer from 1) the inter-vehicle influence during the multi-vehicle interaction, which makes it hard to determine the causality of surrounding vehicles' behavior in controller modeling, or 2) being dominated by the worst-case analysis, which may lead to overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we extend the prior work on Parametric-Control Barrier Function (Parametric-CBF) to multi-robot interactions with embedded causality inference to explicitly reason over the inter-vehicle influence. Given the learned Causality-based Parametric-CBF, we present an adaptive safety-critical controller that allows the ego vehicle to safely react to surrounding vehicles with the learned expectation. We demonstrate that by leveraging the motion flexibility among multi-vehicle systems, task efficiency can be greatly improved in various interaction-intensive scenarios.

</details>


### [140] [Memory Retrieval in Visuomotor Policies for Long-Horizon Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25136)
*Rutav Shah,Yisu Li,Femi Bello,Yuke Zhu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO是一种结合视觉语言模型先验和稀疏注意力机制的视觉运动策略，通过注意力记忆检索机制实现长时域控制，能够在部分可观测环境中从最多8分钟的过去经验中检索任务相关信息，解决了模仿学习中虚假相关性和记忆累积误差两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人在部分可观测环境（如家庭）中需要记忆支持自主性，必须能回忆多种过去信息（物体位置、人类任务完成状态、电器开关时间等）。现有Transformer长上下文注意力机制虽能学习通用检索，但直接用于离线数据模仿学习会导致两个问题：(1) 策略学习到过去信息与预测动作之间的虚假相关性；(2) 预测误差及其与环境交互的累积导致模型漂移和级联故障。

Method: HALO提出两个核心组件：(1) 通过视觉语言模型（VLM）先验蒸馏来抑制虚假相关性——从示范轨迹生成记忆相关的问答对，并与视频问答目标联合训练，引导检索朝向任务相关信息；(2) 使用稀疏注意力限制检索仅访问历史中最相关的部分，减少闭环控制中记忆累积误差的影响。

Result: HALO能够从最多8分钟的过去经验中可靠检索任务相关信息，实现更可靠的长时域控制。

Conclusion: 通过VLM先验蒸馏和稀疏注意力机制，HALO有效解决了直接使用Transformer注意力进行记忆检索时的虚假相关性和误差累积问题，为通用机器人在部分可观测环境中的长时域自主控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: General-purpose robots operating in partially observable environments, such as homes, require memory to support autonomy. They must recall diverse information from the past, such as where objects were placed, which tasks a human partner has completed, and when an appliance was turned on. Achieving this versatility requires a general memory retrieval mechanism. Transformer architectures that use attention over long contexts for memory retrieval provide a promising approach, as they learn retrieval from data rather than relying on task-specific or hand-designed rules. However, directly incorporating them into imitation learning from offline data introduces two key challenges: (1) the policy may learn spurious correlations between past information and predicted actions, and (2) errors accumulate in memory due to prediction inaccuracies and their compounding interactions with the environment, causing model drift and cascading failures. To address both challenges, we introduce HALO, a visuomotor policy with an attention-based memory retrieval mechanism for long-horizon control. First, to suppress spurious correlations, HALO distills vision-language model (VLM) priors into the policy. It generates memory-dependent question--answer pairs from demonstration trajectories and trains jointly with a video question--answering objective, steering retrieval toward task-relevant information. Second, to reduce the impact of accumulated errors in memory during closed-loop control, HALO uses sparse attention that restricts retrieval to only the most relevant parts of the history. Together, these components enable more reliable long-horizon control by guiding the policy to retrieve task-relevant information from up to eight minutes of past experience. Project website: https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/HALO

</details>


### [141] [SwarmFly: A simulation platform for UAV swarm experiment design and validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25146)
*Abhishek Phadke,Karthik Kumar Vasudeva,Abhishek Joshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SwarmFly，一个基于MATLAB的多无人机集群模块化仿真与测试平台，包含四种协同模式、插件架构和八种内置插件，通过八项实验验证了其有效性，并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机集群开源仿真工具存在维护不足、学习曲线陡峭、场景单一等问题，亟需一个全面、模块化的仿真平台来填补这一研究空白。

Method: SwarmFly基于MATLAB开发，集成实时操作地图、四种集群协同模式（领航-跟随、去中心化、异构中继、异构速度）、模拟IMU遥测和IP地理定位，采用插件架构允许研究者在不修改核心代码的情况下添加行为、故障模型和分析工具。平台内置八个插件扩展为完整测试工具。

Result: 通过八项实验验证和表征了各子系统，实验测量了编队精度、抗风能力、故障恢复、能量续航和空域合规性等指标。平台完全运行于MATLAB环境。

Conclusion: SwarmFly的模块化设计支持向硬件在环测试、更大规模集群和更高保真度动力学仿真扩展，已开源发布。

Abstract: The initial development phase of UAV swarms largely depends on simulation for experimental design and validation, yet existing open-source tools are often unmaintained, have steep learning curves, or are built around a single fixed scenario. The need for a comprehensive, modular simulation platform is a recognized research gap. This paper presents SwarmFly, a MATLAB-based simulation and test platform for multi- UAV swarms that addresses these gaps. SwarmFly combines a real-time operational map, four swarm coordination modes (leader-follower, decentralized, heterogeneous relay, and heterogeneous speed), simulated IMU telemetry, and IP-based geolocation with a plugin architecture that lets researchers add behaviors, fault models, and analysis tools without touching the core code. Eight bundled plugins extend the base simulator into a full test harness. The SwarmFly platform exposes multi-agent aerial swarms to a wide range of internal and external disruptions, enabling observation and quantification of underlying swarm control and behavioral mechanisms. This study verifies and characterizes each subsystem through eight experiments that measure formation accuracy, wind tolerance, fault recovery, energy endurance, and airspace compliance. The platform runs entirely in MATLAB. Its modular design supports straightforward extension toward hardware-in-the-loop testing, larger swarms, and higher-fidelity dynamics. An open-source release is available at [https://github.com/abhishekphadke/SwarmFly.git]

</details>


### [142] [Toward Low-Latency Vision-Language Models with Doubly-Correct Predictions in Egocentric Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25160)
*Qitong Wang,Fan Du,Pranav Maneriker,Jihui Jin,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在人体机器人协作任务中的剪枝问题，提出了一种基于证据对齐的剪枝策略，在保持模型高效推理的同时实现"双重正确"（预测正确+证据正确），实验表明该方法优于现有剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM剪枝方法虽然能缩小模型规模、加速推理，但往往只关注预测准确性，忽略了安全人机交互中对可解释性和证据可靠性的需求。作者希望剪枝后的VLM不仅能做出正确预测，还能定位到正确的证据（即双重正确预测），以降低风险、增强用户信任。

Method: 提出了一种基于证据信息的剪枝策略（rationale-informed pruning strategy），通过将模型的证据定位与决策过程对齐，在剪枝过程中保留对正确证据敏感的参数，从而在压缩模型的同时维持双重正确预测能力。

Result: 在以自我为中心的（egocentric）视频数据集上，所提方法在预测准确率和双重正确预测率两方面均优于现有剪枝方法。实验还发现现有剪枝方法往往能保留正确的证据定位但损害预测正确性。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁在追求VLM高效推理的同时，应重视透明性、可审计性和安全性，确保模型在准确性与可解释性之间取得平衡，以支持负责任的人机交互和具身智能发展。

Abstract: The rapid rise of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in egocentric visual understanding has made low-latency inference in human-robot collaborative (HRC) tasks increasingly critical.
