Our architecture integrates a Cross-Attention mechanism to extract structural features from noisy depth data, alongside a Spatial-aware Recurrent Unit (SRU) that maintains robust spatio-temporal memory to mitigate perception blind spots. To bridge the sim-to-real gap in depth perception, we propose a high-fidelity sim-to-real depth sensor modeling pipeline that faithfully replicates real-world sensor artifacts. Additionally, we employ a 3D waypoint-guided active perception reward for proactive sensing, alongside hollow gap kinematic and stair edge penalties to ensure precise foothold placement. We successfully deployed StairMaster on a Unitree Go2 robot, demonstrating its ability to conquer hollow stairs with an unprecedented incline of up to 55$^\circ$ through zero-shot transfer. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first RL-based policy to achieve such steep hollow stair climbing in real-world environments.
  Project Website: https://sivan666666.github.io/StairMaster/.

</details>


### [179] [MIL-LC: A Robust Magnetometer-Inertial-LiDAR Fusion Multimodal Localization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25796)
*Qiyang Lyu,Zhenyu Wu,Wei Wang,Hongming Shen,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种磁力计-惯性-激光雷达融合的多模态定位框架MIL-LC，用于解决GNSS拒绝、几何重复或无纹理场景下自主移动机器人的鲁棒定位问题。该方法不依赖几何/纹理特征或基础设施信标，利用环境磁场作为互补模态，在激光雷达几何退化或磁场图变化时仍能提供可靠定位。仿真与真实实验验证了其鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一模态定位方法受限于传感器固有约束，多模态融合框架仍过度依赖几何/纹理特征或基础设施信标（增加成本且降低部署灵活性）。虽然环境磁场定位作为一种无需几何特征和基础设施的互补模态引起关注，但现有研究仅在行人场景中使用智能手机传感器，尚未有面向自主移动机器人的实用方案。

Method: 设计了MIL-LC框架，包含定制的磁力计-惯性-激光雷达传感器套件，通过多模态融合实现定位。该框架在激光雷达因环境几何退化而失效时，或长期部署中磁场图发生变化时，仍能维持可靠定位性能。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，MIL-LC框架实现了鲁棒且准确的定位性能。

Conclusion: MIL-LC框架有效弥补了现有AMR定位方法在退化环境中的不足，利用环境磁场作为互补模态，在不增加基础设施成本的前提下提升了定位鲁棒性，为AMR在复杂环境中的可靠部署提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Localization in challenging environments, such as GNSS-denied, geometrically repetitive, or textureless scenes commonly found in offices, hotels, and underground parking facilities, remains an open problem for reliable autonomous mobile robot (AMR) deployment. Single-modality localization methods are inherently limited by the constraints of individual sensors. Although multimodal fusion frameworks have shown improved robustness, most existing approaches still rely heavily on geometric or texture features, or on infrastructure-based beacons, which increase installation and maintenance costs while reducing deployment flexibility. Recently, ambient magnetic field (AMF)-based localization has attracted growing attention because it does not depend on geometric or texture features, nor does it require additional infrastructure, making it a promising complementary modality for AMR localization. However, existing studies have only explored such fusion in pedestrian scenarios using smartphone-mounted sensor suites, and practical solutions for AMR systems remain largely unexplored. To address this gap, this article proposes a magnetometer-inertial-LiDAR fused multimodal localization framework with a custom-designed sensor suite, termed MIL-LC, which provides reliable localization even when LiDAR suffers from geometric degeneration or when the magnetic map changes during long-term deployment. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that the proposed MIL-LC framework achieves robust and accurate localization performance.

</details>


### [180] [Beyond a Shadow of a Doubt: Close Proximity Geometry Reconstruction Using FMCW Radar Shadow Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25829)
*Felix de Trogoff du Boisguezennec,Benjamin Ramtoula,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用车辆底盘对雷达波的遮挡形成的几何阴影，通过解析闭式映射从2D雷达扫描中恢复附近细长垂直物体的3D倾斜角度，拓展了毫米波雷达在3D场景重建中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣条件下摄像头和LiDAR性能退化，而毫米波FMCW雷达虽鲁棒但存在垂直方向分辨率不足（elevation collapse）的问题，限制了其几何推理能力。作者观察到车辆底盘遮挡雷达波形成一致的几何阴影，这一线索可用于推断物体的3D信息。

Method: 通过分析底盘阴影的几何一致性，建立了雷达回波边界与物体倾斜角度之间的解析闭式映射关系，无需场景先验假设即可直接计算物体在平面内的3D倾斜角。

Result: 在Navtech CTS350-X雷达仿真与实验中验证了方法的有效性，倾斜角度可在实际条件下被估计，主要瓶颈为雷达扫描中物体的分割精度。

Conclusion: 底盘阴影可作为新颖的几何线索，将2D旋转雷达的角色从定位扩展到3D场景重建，为恶劣环境下的感知提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reliable perception in adverse conditions remains challenging for autonomous systems, as cameras and LiDAR degrade in poor lighting or weather. Millimetre-wave FMCW radar is robust to such conditions, but its elevation collapse limits geometric reasoning. We observe that vehicle chassis occlude radar rays and form a distinctive geometric shadow, and its consistency can enable us to infer useful information about objects whose returns intersect this shadow. Motivated by this observation, we propose a method to recover the 3D, in-plane inclination of nearby slender vertical objects from this cue. The object inclination is retrieved without assumptions about the wider scene, but through an analytical, closed-form mapping between its radar return boundaries and the opening angle. Validation in simulation and experimentation on a Navtech CTS350-X radar shows that inclinations can be estimated under practical conditions, with segmentation of the object in the radar scan emerging as the main bottleneck. This work highlights chassis shadows as a novel geometric cue, extending the role of 2D rotating radar beyond localisation and toward 3D scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [181] [TacVerse: A Multi-Sensor Dataset and Benchmark for Cross-Sensor Vision-Based Tactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25877)
*Lan Wei,Gurmeher Khurana,Sirine Bhouri,Wenhao Hong,Zeyuan Xin,Qingzheng Cong,Wen Fan,Yanzheng Xiang,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacVerse是一个包含106,800张来自七种视觉触觉传感器（VBTSs）的触觉图像的多传感器数据集与基准，支持形状分类、光栅分类和力回归三项任务。实验表明跨传感器直接迁移会导致性能显著下降，而少样本适应能改善但无法完全弥合与同传感器性能的差距，MAE预训练在跨任务和传感器中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉触觉传感器通过内部相机成像形变来推断接触几何和力信息，但不同传感器设计之间的泛化能力尚不明确，缺乏系统性的跨传感器触觉感知研究。

Method: 构建TacVerse多传感器数据集（含7种VBTSs、106,800张触觉图像），设计三种下游任务（形状分类、光栅分类、力回归），在三种实验设置（同传感器训练、零样本跨传感器迁移、少样本适应）下进行评估，并分析MAE自监督预训练的效果。

Result: 同传感器训练在所有任务上表现良好；零样本跨传感器迁移性能显著下降，形状分类相对鲁棒而光栅分类和力回归对传感器偏移更敏感；少样本适应能提升力回归在目标传感器上的表现但未达到同传感器上界；MAE预训练在跨任务和传感器中提供最一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: TacVerse为研究触觉感知中的传感器偏移、数据高效适应和自监督学习提供了受控测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTSs) enable robots to infer contact geometry and force-related cues by imaging deformation through an internal camera, yet generalisation across sensor designs remains poorly understood. We present TacVerse, a multi-sensor dataset and benchmark for cross-sensor vision-based tactile perception. The dataset contains 106,800 tactile images from seven VBTSs and supports three downstream tasks: shape classification, grating classification, and force regression. Experiments are conducted under three settings: within-sensor training, zero-shot cross-sensor transfer, and few-shot adaptation. Strong within-sensor performance across all tasks indicates that the collected tactile observations are informative for the target objectives. Direct cross-sensor transfer, however, leads to substantial degradation. Shape classification is comparatively robust, whereas grating classification and force regression are more sensitive to sensor shift. Few-shot adaptation for force regression consistently improves performance on unseen target sensors but does not fully close the gap to within-sensor upper bounds. A representation study further shows that MAE (Masked Autoencoder) pretraining provides the most consistent gains across tasks and sensors. TacVerse provides a controlled testbed for studying sensor shift, data-efficient adaptation, and self-supervised learning in tactile perception.

</details>


### [182] [A 3D-Printable Dataset for Fair Testing and Comparisons of Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25886)
*Dexter R. Shepherd,Nicolas Herzig,Phil Husbands,Andrew Philippides,Chris Johnson,William Kimbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于数学定义、可3D打印的触觉纹理数据集，包含六种参数化表面图案，并评估了不同打印机和打印材料下的可重复性，建立了首个开放、物理可复现的3D打印触觉纹理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉纹理数据集大多依赖特定传感器与可用表面的交互数据，而非对纹理本身的描述，限制了传感器间的公平比较，阻碍了可重复研究。

Method: 设计六种基于正弦波和傅里叶函数的参数化表面图案，在三种常见3D打印机和多种打印材料上制造，使用光学TacTip传感器在受控接触条件下采集图像，并通过神经网络和PCA模型进行分类实验。

Result: 打印质量（尤其是峰值锐度和拉丝现象）影响触觉方差，高端打印机产生更一致的信号；高质量打印支持良好的内打印机泛化，但跨打印机泛化因几何不一致性仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 建立了首个公开可用、物理可复现的3D打印触觉纹理基准，为触觉传感器的公平比较奠定了基础。

Abstract: Existing texture datasets for tactile sensing primarily consist of sensor readings from a specific sensor interacting with available surfaces/objects rather than describing the textures themselves, limiting fair comparison between tactile sensors and hindering reproducible research. In this work, we introduce a 3D-printable dataset of mathematically defined textures designed to be fabricated reliably across different printers and filament types. The dataset consists of six parametrically generated surface patterns derived from combinations of sine-wave and Fourier-based functions, giving controlled variation in spatial frequency, amplitude, and directional structure. We evaluate the reproducibility of these textures across three popular 3D printers and multiple filament types by measuring variance in images captured using an optical TacTip sensor under controlled contact conditions. Our results show that print quality, particularly peak sharpness and stringing, affects tactile variance, with higher-end printers producing significantly more consistent signatures. Classification experiments using neural networks and PCA-based models further demonstrate that high-quality prints support strong within-printer generalisation, while cross-printer generalisation remains challenging due to geometric inconsistencies. This work establishes the first openly available, physically reproducible 3D-printed texture benchmark, providing a foundation for fair comparison of tactile sensors.

</details>


### [183] [DeformGen: Dynamics-Based Topology Augmentation for Deformable Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25939)
*Zili Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Yuyang Zhang,Zekun Qi,Junyan Lin,Hanxin Zhu,Jiaolong Yang,Zhibo Chen,Yao Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeformGen提出了一种基于动力学的数据增强框架，通过对可变形物体施加局部物理扰动并前向模拟来扩展有效状态分布，同时利用变形场扭曲将操作轨迹与变形几何对齐，从而联合增强状态分布及其操作行为，显著提升可变形操作策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演示增强方法在可变形操作中面临两个根本性挑战：(1)高维状态空间受物理约束，低维位姿扰动无法生成有效构型；(2)轨迹传递非等变性，变形后材料点不再刚性地一起运动。需要一种能够实现拓扑多样性的增强框架。

Method: DeformGen包含两个核心模块：(1)状态增强：施加局部物理扰动并前向仿真动力学，获得拓扑一致且物理合理的可变形状态；(2)轨迹增强：通过变形场扭曲将源操作轨迹映射到变形后几何上，将逐粒子位移提升为连续空间函数，使末端执行器轨迹与变形几何自适应对齐。

Result: 在高保真可变形操作基准测试中，DeformGen相比仅使用原始演示训练以及刚性风格增强基线，普遍提升了策略学习效果。

Conclusion: DeformGen通过动力学驱动的联合状态-轨迹增强框架，有效解决了可变形操作中数据增强的拓扑多样性和物理合理性难题，为可变形操作策略学习提供了一种通用且高效的增强范式。

Abstract: Demonstration augmentation is proposed for cost-efficient data acquisition, but existing methods are fundamentally limited in deformable manipulation due to two challenges: (1) the state space is high-dimensional with physics-induced constraints, making valid configurations impossible to reach via low-dimensional pose perturbations; and (2) trajectory transfer is non-equivariant, as material points no longer move rigidly together under deformation. We present DeformGen, a dynamics-based augmentation framework that achieves topological diversity for deformable objects. For the state challenge, DeformGen expands the valid state distribution by applying localized physical disturbances and forward-simulating the dynamics to obtain topology-coherent, physically plausible deformable states. For the trajectory challenge, DeformGen transfers source manipulation trajectories via deformation-field warping, which lifts per-particle displacements into a continuous spatial function to adapt the end-effector trajectory consistently with the deformed geometry. In this way, our method jointly augments the state distribution and its associated manipulation behavior. Experiments on high-fidelity deformable manipulation benchmarks show that DeformGen generally improves policy learning compared with training on the original demonstrations alone and with rigid-style augmentation baselines.

</details>


### [184] [DSP-SLAM++: A Unified Framework for Multi-Class, High-Fidelity Object SLAM in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25953)
*Ahmad Kourani,Ghina Daoud,Daniel Asmar,Imad Elhajj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DSP-SLAM++ 通过异步建图流水线和单目鱼眼-LiDAR传感器融合适配，在保持实时性能的同时实现多类物体高保真、几何完整形状重建，将最大物体处理延迟降低70%，使高保真多类物体SLAM更适用于自动驾驶和机器人操作等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体感知SLAM系统在实时性、多类支持和生成高保真语义连贯物体模型之间存在权衡，亟需一种能同时兼顾三者性能的方法。

Method: 提出DSP-SLAM++，在DSP-SLAM框架基础上引入异步建图流水线实现实时性能，并针对单目鱼眼-LiDAR传感器套件进行专用传感器融合适配。

Result: 系统支持多类物体精细几何完整形状重建，最大物体处理延迟相比最先进基线降低高达70%，在具有挑战性的25Hz多类数据集上实现鲁棒的实时性能。

Conclusion: 本工作通过支持常见的鱼眼-LiDAR传感器平台，使高保真多类物体SLAM更实用化，推动了其在自动驾驶和机器人操作等实际场景中的应用。

Abstract: Existing object-aware SLAM systems force a trade-off between real-time performance, multi-class support, and the generation of high-fidelity, semantically coherent object models. To address this trade-off, we present DSP-SLAM++, which extends the DSP-SLAM framework with an asynchronous mapping pipeline for real-time performance and dedicated sensor fusion adaptations for a monocular fisheye-LiDAR suite. Experiments demonstrate that our system generates fine-grained, geometrically-complete shapes for multiple object classes while eliminating severe mapping thread bottlenecks by reducing maximum object processing latency by up to 70\% compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, enabling robust, real-time performance on a challenging 25 Hz multi-class datasets. This work makes high-fidelity, multi-class object SLAM more practical for real-world applications like autonomous driving and robotic manipulation by enabling its use on platforms with common fisheye-LiDAR sensor setups. The open-source code is available at: [github.com/AUBVRL/DSP-SLAMpp].

</details>


### [185] [From Rubble Simulation to Active Magnetic Mapping: Quantum Sensing for Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25957)
*Samuel Tovey,Stefan Prestel,Hiroshi Yamauchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于无人机的量子磁力测量技术，通过模拟钢筋混凝土地下车库倒塌场景，结合贝叶斯主动采样和高斯过程回归，证明在坍塌建筑上方约1米处可恢复亚pT至亚nT范围的磁结构，三传感器阵列优化了梯度分辨率与无人机载荷的平衡，约100个采样点即可达到峰值结构相关性，为灾难响应中的空洞检测提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑物倒塌后72小时内的幸存者定位是灾难响应的关键挑战，现有传感模态只能提供废墟下结构的局部信息，需要一种能够穿透废墟进行结构感知的补充性探测手段。

Method: 使用Unreal Engine生成钢筋混凝土地下车库倒塌场景；通过每三角形偶极近似计算感应磁场；采用高斯过程回归作为后端，结合贝叶斯主动采样进行稀疏多传感器数据重建；通过多个独立倒塌实现验证管线性能。

Result: 在倒塌建筑上方约1米处可恢复亚pT至亚nT范围的有意义磁结构；三传感器阵列在梯度分辨率和无人机载荷之间达到最优权衡；主动采样约需100个样本即可达到峰值结构相关性。

Conclusion: 量子级磁传感有潜力成为无人机结构分析和坍塌建筑空洞检测的有效工具，为灾难响应提供补充性探测能力。

Abstract: Locating survivors of building collapses within the first 72 hours is a critical challenge in disaster response, and existing sensing modalities provide only partial information about the structure beneath the rubble.
  This paper proposes drone-based quantum magnetometry as a complementary modality and develops a simulation pipeline spanning rubble physics, sensor-array deployment, and active spatial reconstruction. We use Unreal Engine to generate a steel-reinforced concrete parking-garage collapse and compute the induced magnetic field via a per-triangle dipole approximation, establishing that meaningful magnetic structure is recoverable in the sub-pT to sub-nT range from roughly 1 m above the roofline. Then, we feed sparse multi-sensor samples into a Gaussian Process Regression back-end driven by Bayesian active sampling and validate the pipeline across multiple independent collapse realizations; a three-sensor array optimizes the trade-off between gradient resolution and UAV payload constraints, and active sampling reaches peak structural correlation in roughly $100$ samples. Together, these results indicate that quantum-grade sensing could become a useful tool for drone-based structural analysis and potentially void detection in collapsed buildings.

</details>


### [186] [Mixture-of-Experts RL for Fault-Tolerant Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25965)
*Giulio Turrisi,Ozan Pali,Luca Oneto,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种故障感知模块化控制架构，通过显式利用故障诊断信息激活针对不同执行器失效模式的专用控制专家，使腿式机器人在执行器故障和复杂地形下保持可靠运动。实验表明，该模块化策略在性能上优于同规模单一策略，且在计算资源受限场景下仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人部署在行星探测等远程环境中时，必须应对执行器故障和复杂地形挑战。现有强化学习方法虽取得良好效果，但单一策略难以高效编码应对不同故障条件所需的多样化控制策略。

Method: 提出一种故障感知模块化控制架构，显式利用故障诊断信息激活与不同执行器失效模式对应的专用控制专家模块。

Result: 实验表明，显式故障条件化的模块化策略在性能上始终优于同等规模的单一策略，在多种故障场景下均实现更高的运动性能；即使网络容量大幅降低，模块化架构仍能保持竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构适用于计算资源受限的机器人平台（如空间应用中常用的平台），代码已开源。

Abstract: Legged robots deployed in planetary exploration and other remote environments must maintain reliable locomotion despite actuator failures and challenging terrain conditions. Although reinforcement learning has achieved strong results in legged locomotion, monolithic policies can struggle to efficiently represent the diverse control strategies required to compensate for different fault conditions. In this work, we propose a fault-aware modular control architecture that explicitly leverages fault-diagnosis information to activate specialized control experts associated with distinct actuator failure modes. Experimental results show that explicit fault-conditioned modular policies consistently outperform monolithic policies of comparable size, achieving higher locomotion performance across failure scenarios. Moreover, the proposed modular architecture retains competitive performance even under significantly reduced network capacity, highlighting its suitability for compute-constrained robotic platforms, such as those typically employed in space applications. The code associated with this work is available at: https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/fault-locomotion-isaaclab.

</details>


### [187] [A Sensorised Lattice Footplate for a Semi-Active Prosthetic Foot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25966)
*Jinze Ge,Jingcheng Sun,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了将磁性足底传感嵌入低成本半主动假脚承重弹性元件内部的可行性，通过集成3D打印网格足板、伺服可调液压阻尼器和降阶踝关节模型的原型，验证了传感内嵌及半主动阻尼控制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将磁性足底传感直接嵌入假脚承重弹性元件内部，以替代传统外部鞋垫式传感层，实现更紧凑、低成本的植入力传感方案，并驱动半主动阻尼控制。

Method: 1) 设计集成传感器化3D打印网格足板、伺服可调液压阻尼器和降阶踝关节模型的原型；2) 实验表征阻尼器调节角度与阻尼系数的关系；3) 进行压缩控制试验验证网格刚度可调性；4) 循环法向加载试验验证嵌入式传感器追踪测试机参考力的能力；5) 静态姿态试验验证前足与后足载荷分布的可分离性；6) 设计前馈阻尼调度跟踪参考踝关节背屈轨迹。

Result: 1) 网格刚度在压缩控制试验中显示可调性；2) 嵌入式传感器能追踪测试机参考力，支持无需外部鞋垫层的足底力估计；3) 静态姿态下前足和后足载荷分布在四种预设站立构型中可分离；4) 前馈阻尼调度可近似参考踝关节早期至中期背屈趋势，但纯耗散机制无法产生主动蹬伸。

Conclusion: 传感可内嵌于假脚承重弹性元件内部，并用于驱动半主动阻尼控制，但纯耗散机制存在无法产生主动蹬伸的固有限制。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether magnetic plantar sensing can be embedded directly inside the load-bearing compliant element of a low-cost semi-active prosthetic foot. We present a prototype integrating a sensorised 3D-printed lattice footplate, a servo-adjustable hydraulic damper, and a reduced-order ankle model. The damper is experimentally characterised to relate adjustment angle to damping coefficient. Controlled compression tests show tunable lattice stiffness, while cyclic normal loading shows that the embedded sensor tracks the testing-machine reference force, supporting plantar-force estimation without an external insole layer. Static-posture trials under approximately body-weight loading show that forefoot and rearfoot loading distributions are separable across four prescribed stance configurations, providing a preliminary check of the sensing pipeline. A feedforward damping schedule approximates the dorsiflexion trend of a reference ankle trajectory through early-to-mid stance, while exposing the expected limitation that a purely dissipative mechanism cannot generate active push-off. Together, these results demonstrate that sensing can be embedded inside the load-bearing compliant element of a prosthetic foot and used to drive semi-active damping.

</details>


### [188] [Action ControlNet: A Lightweight Delay-Aware Adapter for Smooth Asynchronous Control in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25985)
*Tiecheng Guo,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Action ControlNet (ACNet)，一种轻量级延迟感知适配器，通过将已执行的运动后缀作为残差条件应用于冻结的动作头，解决VLA模型在异步执行中的推断延迟和块拼接不连续问题，提升鲁棒性和轨迹平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人操作中表现优异，但其推理延迟阻碍了稳定的高频控制。异步执行虽可缓解延迟，但会导致动作块拼接不连续、抖动及接触式操作失败，现有方案需全策略重训或特定架构运行时逻辑，缺乏轻量通用解决方案。

Method: 提出ACNet，一种延迟感知适配器，利用已执行的运动后缀作为残差条件，作用于大部分冻结的动作头。该方法保持预训练骨干网络不变，引入少量可训练参数，兼容扩散模型和流匹配等生成式动作头。

Result: 在Kinetix、Meta-World MT50及真实SO-ARM101平台上，ACNet在推理延迟下显著提升鲁棒性，生成比直接块拼接更平滑的异步轨迹，同时比全延迟条件重训更轻量。

Conclusion: ACNet通过轻量适配有效解决了VLA模型异步执行中的延迟与不连续性难题，为高频机器人控制提供了一个无需重训骨干网络、兼容多种生成式动作头的实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation, but their inference latency remains a major obstacle to stable high-frequency control. Asynchronous execution mitigates this bottleneck by overlapping policy inference with action execution, yet the next action chunk is still predicted from stale observations while the robot continues to move. Direct chunk stitching therefore introduces handoff discontinuities, action jitter, and failures in contact-rich manipulation. Existing remedies typically require either full-policy retraining or architecture-specific runtime logic. This work proposes Action ControlNet (ACNet), a lightweight delay-aware adapter that uses the executed motion suffix as a residual condition for a mostly frozen action head. ACNet leaves the pretrained backbone unchanged, introduces few trainable parameters, and remains compatible with generative action heads such as diffusion and flow matching. On Kinetix, Meta-World MT50, and a real-world SO-ARM101 platform, ACNet improves robustness under inference delay and yields smoother asynchronous trajectories than direct chunk stitching, while remaining more lightweight than full delay-conditioned retraining.

</details>


### [189] [FORCE: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Fine-Tuning via Value-Calibrated Warm-up and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26006)
*Shuyi Zhang,Yunfan Lou,Hongyang Cheng,Yichen Guo,Chuyao Fu,Yaoxu Lyu,Xiaojie Zhang,Haoran Li,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FORCE是一个三阶段框架，通过值校准预热和在线阶段的高价值动作过滤机制，解决了VLA模型强化学习微调中的样本效率低下和初始灾难性遗忘问题，在仿真和真实任务中取得79%的成功率提升，且无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于次优数据的模仿天花板，RL微调虽能突破但样本效率极低，原因在于Q函数不稳定导致的初始灾难性遗忘和低质量探索数据导致的低效策略更新，迫使依赖昂贵的人工干预。

Method: FORCE包含三个阶段：(1) 值校准预热阶段，利用同策略轨迹缓解Q函数的分布偏移；(2) 在线阶段，将校准后的Q函数作为过滤器，仅保留高价值动作用于策略更新，同时过滤策略自身提议和专家数据。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实世界任务中，FORCE实现了79%的成功率绝对提升，相比先前的RL方法提升10%，训练速度加快32.5%，且消除了常见的成功率下降问题，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: FORCE通过稳定的值函数校准和基于价值的动作过滤机制，显著提升了VLA模型RL微调的样本效率和性能，向部署高能力自主机器人代理迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often constrained by the imitation ceiling imposed by sub-optimal data. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning can surpass this limit, it is notoriously sample inefficient. This challenge arises from two core issues: (1) catastrophic initial unlearning due to an unstable Q-function and (2) inefficient policy updates caused by low-quality exploration data, often forcing a reliance on costly human interventions. We introduce FORCE, a 3-stage framework that stabilizes fine-tuning by tackling both issues. FORCE first incorporates a Value-Calibrated Warm-Up phase, utilizing on-policy rollouts to mitigate the distributional shift of the Q-function. Subsequently, during the online stage, this calibrated Q-function acts as a filter for both the policy's own action proposals and expert data, ensuring only high-value actions are used for the policy update. We evaluate FORCE on various simulation and real-world tasks, and the result shows that FORCE achieves a 79% absolute improvement in success rates and outperform prior RL methods by 10%, while accelerating training by 32.5%. Critically, it mitigates the common success rate drop and achieves this robust performance without human intervention, marking a significant step towards deploying capable and autonomous robotic agents.

</details>


### [190] [Emcar: Embodied Controller for Animating Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26008)
*Carlos Gomez Cubero,Elizabeth Jochum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMCAR是一个利用木偶戏和绘画等艺术实践来编程机器人运动的新型无代码软件工具，使没有技术背景的艺术家也能参与人机交互研究和协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人编程工具对非技术背景用户（尤其是艺术家）不友好，限制了创造性的人机交互应用。需要一种更直观、包容的编程方式，让艺术家能够直接参与机器人交互设计与研究。

Method: EMCAR平台采用无代码方式，借鉴木偶戏和绘画等艺术实践的独特可供性（affordances），允许用户通过艺术化的直观操作来设计和编程机器人运动。

Result: 该平台扩展了协作机器人的创造性应用范围，使机器人直接掌握在艺术家手中，并为缺乏技术背景的个人提供了有意义的参与机器人研究和协作的途径。

Conclusion: EMCAR通过融合艺术实践与机器人编程，降低了人机交互的技术门槛，创造了更具包容性的机器人研究环境，推动了HRI领域的创造性应用发展。

Abstract: This chapter describes EMCAR, a novel software tool for programming robot motion that leverages the unique affordances of artistic practices such as puppetry and drawing to conceive, design, and program novel interactions and realize new use cases for HRI. The advantage of this no-code platform is that it expands creative applications for collaborative robots - putting robots directly in the hands of artists - and provides an inclusive environment that enables individuals with little or no technical backgrounds to engage meaningfully in collaborations and robotics research.

</details>


### [191] [FAR-LIO: Enabling High-Speed Autonomy through Fast, Accurate, and Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26010)
*Maximilian Leitenstern,Marcel Weinmann,Patrick Haft,Tobias Lasser,Dominik Kulmer,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAR-LIO，一个基于CUDA加速的激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过新颖的体素哈希图、稀疏感知GICP算法和EKF后端，在高速自主赛车场景中实现快速、准确且鲁棒的位姿估计，相比SOTA平均降低6.9%位置误差和38.4%运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高动态运动和传感器噪声环境下（如高速自主赛车），里程计估计面临挑战，而最小化里程计延迟对于稳定闭环控制至关重要。现有方法在精度、速度和鲁棒性上难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 基于CUDA的体素哈希图实现并行化近邻搜索和高效地图更新；2) 稀疏感知GICP算法结合自适应阈值，在CUDA体素哈希图上实现低延迟高精度；3) EKF后端采用上采样和延迟补偿策略融合高频IMU数据，保证鲁棒平滑的里程计输出。

Result: 在四种不同传感器配置下（包括两辆时速高达250 km/h的自主赛车）进行测试，相比SOTA基线，位置误差平均降低6.9%，运行时间降低38.4%，且使用单一参数集。

Conclusion: FAR-LIO展示了卓越的计算效率和广泛适用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Robust and accurate odometry estimation is essential in modern robotics. In environments characterized by highly dynamic motion and sensor noise, odometry estimation becomes increasingly challenging. Autonomous racing combines both factors in an unstructured setting, where minimizing odometry latency is essential for stable closed-loop control. This paper introduces FAR-LIO, a highly optimized CUDA-accelerated LiDAR-inertial odometry framework developed for Fast, Accurate, and Robust performance. Our system leverages a novel CUDA-based voxel hashmap to enable parallelized nearest-neighbor search and efficient map updates. We employ a sparsity-aware Generalized Iterative Closest Point algorithm with adaptive thresholding on top of the CUDA-based voxel hashmap with adaptive density to achieve low-latency without compromising accuracy. An Extended Kalman Filter serves as a robust backend. It utilizes an upsampling and delay compensation strategy to fuse the LiDAR odometry with high-frequency IMU data, thereby ensuring a robust and smooth odometry output. We evaluate FAR-LIO across four different sensor setups, using both public datasets and data from two autonomous racecars driving at speeds of up to 250 km/h. FAR-LIO achieves an average 6.9% reduction in the positional error and 38.4% lower runtime compared to state-of-the-art baselines on target hardware using a single parameter set. This demonstrates its computational efficiency and broad applicability. To build upon our work, our code is available open-source on https://github.com/TUMFTM/FAR-LIO.

</details>


### [192] [G2DP: Diffusion Planning with Spatio-Temporal Grid Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26017)
*Hang Yu,Ye Jin,Alessandro Canevaro,Julian Schmidt,Julian Jordan,Peizheng Li,Marc Kaufeld,Silvan Lindner,Johannes Betz,Wilhelm Stork*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G2DP，一种基于扩散模型的自动驾驶规划器，通过构建可微的时空代价体积在推理时注入密集环境约束，有效引导轨迹生成，在nuPlan等基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散规划器存在随机性，需要显式引导确保安全与路径遵守，而现有引导方法（稀疏几何查询或事后细化）情境感知有限，交互场景下表现脆弱。

Method: 构建可微的时空代价体积（融合概率性未来占据分布与路线进度图），将其公式化为连续安全能量泛函，在去噪循环中注入密集梯度，主动引导轨迹生成向无碰撞且进度最优的区域移动。

Result: 在nuPlan上比最强模仿学习基线提升+7.2反应分数；零样本迁移至interPlan和DeepScenario保持最高分，在interPlan上碰撞避免比无引导方法提升+10.15。

Conclusion: 时空代价网格可作为扩散规划器中鲁棒引导的有效表示。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, diffusion-based planners have emerged as a promising paradigm for robust motion planning in dense and interactive traffic, as they can effectively model diverse driving behaviors. However, their inherent stochasticity often requires explicit guidance during denoising to ensure safety and route adherence for robust closed-loop execution. Existing guidance typically relies on sparse, entity-centric geometric queries or post-hoc refinement, yielding limited situational awareness and fragile performance in interactive scenes. To address this issue, we propose G2DP (Grid-Guided Diffusion Planning), a diffusion-based planner that directly enforces dense environmental constraints through inference-time guidance. Specifically, G2DP constructs a differentiable spatio-temporal cost volume by fusing probabilistic future occupancy distributions with a route-progress map. By formulating this volume as a continuous safety energy functional, it injects dense gradients directly into the denoising loop, actively steering trajectory generation toward collision-free and progress-optimal regions. Extensive closed-loop evaluations show that G2DP achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuPlan, outperforming the strongest imitation-learning baseline by +7.2 points in reactive score. It further maintains top scores in zero-shot transfers to interPlan and DeepScenario benchmarks, with collision avoidance improving by +10.15 over the unguided approach on interPlan. These results demonstrate that spatio-temporal cost grids serve as an effective representation for robust guidance in diffusion-based planning.

</details>


### [193] [In-Context World Modeling for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26025)
*Siyin Wang,Junhao Shi,Senyu Fei,Zhaoyang Fu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ICWM框架，通过任务无关的短交互历史进行上下文系统辨识，使VLA模型无需参数更新即可适应新相机视角和机器人形态等未知配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常仅以当前观测和语言指令为条件，忽略了底层系统配置变量，导致在相机视角改变、机器人形态变化等新场景下泛化能力差，需要大量数据微调才能适应。

Method: 提出In-Context World Modeling (ICWM)框架，将系统辨识转化为上下文自适应问题。在任务执行前，让机器人策略通过一段短历史的自生成、任务无关交互来推断关键系统变量，利用上下文窗口理解系统运行方式，而非传统ICL那样用于指定任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上，ICWM在新相机视角设置下显著优于标准VLA基线。

Conclusion: ICWM通过上下文自适应实现了无需参数更新的系统辨识与动态建模，有效提升了VLA模型对未知环境配置的泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often fail to generalize to novel setups, such as altered camera viewpoints or robot morphologies, because they are typically conditioned only on current observations and language instructions. By ignoring the underlying system configuration as a variable, these models implicitly assume a fixed execution context encountered during training, necessitating data-intensive fine-tuning for any new environment. In this work, we introduce In-Context World Modeling (ICWM), a framework that treats system identification as an in-context adaptation problem. ICWM enables robot policies to autonomously infer essential system variables from a short history of self-generated, task-agnostic interactions. Unlike traditional In-Context Learning that uses demonstrations to specify what task to perform, ICWM leverages the context window to understand how the system operates. By processing these interactions before task execution, the model implicitly captures the world dynamics of the current system, enabling adaptation to novel configurations without parameter updates. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real-world robot platforms demonstrate that ICWM significantly outperforms standard VLA baselines on novel camera viewpoints.

</details>


### [194] [RoboAtlas: Contextual Active SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26046)
*Alexander Schperberg,Shivam K. Panda,Abraham P. Vinod,M. K. Jawed,Stefano Di Cairano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAtlas是一个结合几何探索与语义推理的上下文主动SLAM框架，通过可扩展的3D语义地图系统OpenRoboVox，利用上下文多臂老虎机算法在探索和语义导航间自适应平衡。在1800m²以上的真实环境（约3万语义实例）中实现100%任务成功率，在GOAT-Bench基准上取得90.6%的SOTA成功率（GPT-4o），使用轻量级Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型仍达88.8%，超越所有GPT-4o基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动SLAM方法通常在几何探索和语义推理之间缺乏自适应平衡，且缺乏可扩展的3D语义地图来有效结合大模型能力。本文旨在构建一个能够根据场景理解程度动态调整探索策略的上下文主动SLAM框架。

Method: 提出RoboAtlas框架，包含可扩展3D语义地图系统OpenRoboVox，通过上下文多臂老虎机（contextual multi-armed bandit）算法，融合前沿探索、全局语义地图推理和自我中心VLM推理，实现从探索到语义导航的自适应过渡。

Result: 在1800m²以上真实环境（约3万语义实例）中实现100%任务成功率；GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen"基准上GPT-4o版本SR达90.6%（超越最强基线17.8个百分点），Qwen2.5-VL-7B版本达88.8%（超越所有GPT-4o基线）。

Conclusion: 将基础大模型与大规模3D语义地图相结合，能够实现鲁棒且高效的上下文主动SLAM；语义地图框架所获取的信息比简单地更换底层基础模型更为重要。

Abstract: We present RoboAtlas, a contextual Active SLAM framework that adaptively balances geometric exploration and semantic reasoning using a scalable 3D semantic mapping system, OpenRoboVox. RoboAtlas integrates frontier exploration, global semantic-map reasoning, and egocentric VLM-based reasoning through a contextual multi-armed bandit that transitions from exploration to semantically guided navigation as scene understanding improves. We evaluate the system in simulation and on a Unitree Go2 robot in large-scale real-world environments exceeding 1800 m2 with approx. 30k mapped semantic instances, achieving a 100% task success rate. On the GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen" benchmark, RoboAtlas achieves state-of-the-art performance with highest reported success rate (SR) of 90.6%, using GPT-4o, improving over the strongest prior baseline by 17.8 percentage points in SR. Using the much smaller Qwen2.5-VL-7B model, it still achieves 88.8% SR, outperforming all baselines using GPT-4o in SR, and revealing the importance of the information gained by our semantic mapping framework over simply replacing the underlying foundation model. The results demonstrate that grounding foundation models with large-scale 3D semantic maps enables robust and efficient contextual Active SLAM.

</details>


### [195] [Learning Action Priors for Cross-embodiment Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26095)
*Dong Jing,Tianqi Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Jinman Zhao,Zelong Sun,Li Erran Li,Zhiwu Lu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段训练框架，在VLA模型的动作模块中预训练运动先验，再与VLM交叉模态对齐，使动作模块在视觉-语言训练前就具备跨本体的时序运动结构，从而提升收敛速度、成功率和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型基于VLM骨干网络附加动作模块并联合优化，但动作模块几乎从零学习物理运动，缺乏显式的运动先验，导致早期优化需同时发现时序动作动力学与跨模态对齐，在跨本体场景下问题更突出。

Method: 两阶段训练框架：Stage 1 使用轻量级流匹配编码器-解码器动作模块，仅从无条件的动作轨迹中学习时序运动结构，不处理视觉或语言token；Stage 2 通过解码器复用和早期潜在蒸馏将学到的先验迁移至VLA训练，对齐视觉-语言特征与动作嵌入空间，同时支持端到端策略精调。训练好的编码器还可作为紧凑的历史压缩器，将状态-动作历史汇总为单个时序上下文token。

Result: 在13个跨本体任务（模拟和真实环境）上，相比无动作先验的VLA训练，模型收敛更快、成功率更高，在数据稀缺的真实世界任务上表现显著更强。Stage 1 的动作数据规模扩增能产生更泛化的动作先验，直接提升下游VLA性能。

Conclusion: 在VLA交叉模态对齐前预训练动作模块的运动先验是有效的，能够加速收敛、提升性能和泛化能力，且动作先验可随数据规模扩展而改进。

Abstract: Most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build on a Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone by attaching an action module and optimizing the full policy jointly. This design inherits strong visual and linguistic priors from the VLM, but leaves the action module to learn physical motion almost from scratch. As a result, the policy lacks an explicit motion prior, forcing early optimization to simultaneously discover temporal action dynamics and cross-modal alignment, a challenge further amplified in cross-embodiment settings. In this work, we propose to pretrain the action module with motion priors before cross-modal VLA alignment. Specifically, we introduce a two-stage training framework that equips the action module with cross-embodiment temporal motion structure before VLA training begins. In Stage~1, a lightweight flow-matching-based encoder-decoder action module efficiently learns temporal motion structure solely from unconditioned action trajectories, without processing visual or language tokens. In Stage~2, this learned prior is transferred to VLA training through decoder reuse and early-stage latent distillation, aligning visual-language features with the action embedding space while still allowing end-to-end policy refinement. In addition, the trained encoder serves as a compact history compressor, summarizing state-action histories into a single temporal context token for history-aware modeling at negligible cost. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse cross-embodiment tasks on both simulated and real-world platforms validate the effectiveness of our approach. Compared with VLA training without action priors, our model achieves faster convergence, higher success rates, and substantially stronger performance on data-scarce real-world tasks. Moreover, scaling up the action data in Stage~1 yields a more generalizable action prior that directly improves downstream VLA performance.

</details>


### [196] [Learning Robot Visual Navigation in Crowds via Intention-Aware Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26047)
*Han Bao,Bingyi Xia,Hanjing Ye,Yu Zhan,Hao Cheng,Baozhi Jia,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出iCrowdNav，一种基于视觉的意图感知人群导航方法，通过时空编码器和I² Former模块从自我中心视觉观测中编码行为与结构上下文，用于深度强化学习策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL导航方法将行人简化为2D点，忽略了人类和环境的丰富视觉线索，导致场景表征有限。

Method: 提出iCrowdNav，包含两个关键组件：1）时空编码器提取场景占据特征；2）Intent-Interact Former (I² Former) 注意力模块编码人体姿态以推断行人运动意图。两者融合为紧凑状态嵌入，支持DRL策略训练。

Result: 在广泛实验中，该方法优于基线方法，并在真实环境部署中验证了基于视觉的人群导航能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入意图感知的场景表征，利用视觉线索（人体姿态）和环境结构信息，能够更有效地学习人群导航策略，实现从仿真到真实世界的迁移。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires the ability to infer human intentions while accounting for the structural constraints of the environment. Currently, deep reinforcement learning (DRL) provides a promising method for learning navigation policies that understand human intentions. However, most of them rely on limited scene representations, treating pedestrians as simple 2D points and ignoring rich visual cues from both humans and the environment. To address this issue, we introduce iCrowdNav, a novel visual crowd navigation method with intention-aware scene representations, to encode behavioral and structural context from egocentric visual observations. Our method employs two key components: a spatio-temporal encoder for extracting occupancy features of the scene, and Intent-Interact Former (I$^2$ Former), an attention-based module that encodes human poses to infer pedestrians' motion intentions. These features are integrated into a compact state embedding that supports effective DRL policy training. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves superior performance over baselines, and real-world deployment demonstrates vision-based crowd navigation.

</details>


### [197] [Deep Reinforcement Learning-Enhanced Event-Triggered Data-Driven Predictive Control for a 3D Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26048)
*Cheng Ouyang,Moeen Ul Islam,Kaixiang Zhang,Zhaojian Li,Xiaobo Tan,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应强化学习的事件触发数据驱动预测控制（RL-ET-DeePC）框架，通过训练无模型RL策略判断何时调用DeePC优化器，在保持闭环性能的同时显著降低计算频率。仿真显示优化频率降低66%，硬件实验在三维缆索驱动软体机械臂上实现零样本迁移，优化频率降低34%，跟踪精度与周期DeePC相当且优于静态阈值基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因非线性和时变动力学难以控制。DeePC虽免于建模，但其滚动时域优化在每采样时刻求解约束优化问题，计算负担大，难以在资源受限的机器人平台上实时部署。

Method: 提出RL-ET-DeePC框架：训练一个无模型强化学习（RL）策略，根据当前系统状态表征决定何时触发DeePC优化器，从而减少不必要的优化调用，同时保持闭环性能。

Result: 仿真中优化频率降低66%，跟踪精度与周期DeePC相当；在三维缆索驱动软体机械臂的硬件实验中实现零样本迁移，优化频率降低34%，跟踪精度与周期DeePC相当，且性能一致性优于静态阈值事件触发基线。

Conclusion: RL-ET-DeePC有效降低了DeePC的计算频率，在保持跟踪精度的同时适用于资源受限的软体机器人实时控制，且具备良好的泛化能力（零样本迁移）。

Abstract: Soft robots are challenging to control due to their nonlinear and time-varying dynamics. Data-enabled predictive control (DeePC) offers a model-free alternative by directly leveraging measured input-output trajectories to construct a predictive controller. However, its receding-horizon formulation requires solving a constrained optimization problem at every sampling instant, which can be computationally demanding for real-time deployment on resource-limited robotic platforms.To address this limitation, we propose an adaptive reinforcement-learning-based event-triggered DeePC (RL-ET-DeePC) framework for soft robotic control. A model-free RL policy is trained to determine when to invoke the DeePC optimizer based on the current system state representation, thereby reducing unnecessary optimization calls while preserving closed-loop performance.Simulation results show that RL-ET-DeePC reduces optimization frequency by up to 66% compared to periodic DeePC, while maintaining comparable tracking accuracy. Hardware experiments on a three-dimensional cable-driven soft robotic arm demonstrate zero-shot transfer, achieving a 34% reduction in optimization frequency with tracking accuracy comparable to periodic DeePC and more consistent performance than a static threshold-based event-triggered baseline.

</details>


### [198] [ForceBand: Learning Forceful Manipulation with sEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26093)
*Botao He,Zhi Wang,Linna Kuang,Ishaan Ghosh,Jitendra Malik,Cornelia Fermuller,Tingfan Wu,Jiayuan Mao,Ruoshi Liu,Haozhi Qi,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForceBand，一种低成本腕戴式sEMG系统，通过记录人类前臂肌肉活动为机器人示范添加力信息，使机器人学会需要精细力控制的操纵任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类示范数据（如动捕、视频）主要记录运动轨迹和外观，缺失接触力信息，无法支持对力敏感的机器人操纵任务学习。

Method: 1) 收集10小时多模态数据集（含第一视角视频、sEMG、IMU、指尖力传感器）；2) 预训练EMG2Force模型（从sEMG+IMU预测每指力）；3) 用户通过短时校准后，仅用ForceBand和视频采集任务示范，模型为示范标注指尖力轨迹。

Result: ForceBand的力预测误差比基于视觉的基线降低50%以上；在需要物体特定力控制的抓取、挤压、放置任务中成功率达87%。

Conclusion: ForceBand是一种低成本、有效的力增强示范采集方案，能够使机器人学习需要精细力控制的操纵策略。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are a scalable data source for learning robot manipulation policies. However, common sources of human demonstration data, such as motion-capture trajectories and internet videos, capture mostly motion and appearance while missing the contact forces that are critical for force-sensitive manipulation. In this paper, we introduce ForceBand, a low-cost wrist-worn sEMG system that turns human muscle activity into force-enriched demonstrations. We first collect a 10-hour multimodal dataset containing egocentric video, sEMG, IMU, and fingertip force measurements across diverse actions and objects. Using this dataset, we pre-train an EMG2Force model that predicts per-finger forces from sEMG and IMU signals. After a short user-specific calibration, users can collect target-task demonstrations using only ForceBand and video; EMG2Force then labels these demonstrations with per-finger force traces, producing force-augmented demonstrations for robot policy learning. Experiments show that ForceBand recovers fine-grained fingertip interactions with over 50% lower force prediction error than vision-based baselines and achieves an 87% success rate on pick, squeeze, and place tasks that require object-specific force control across objects with diverse shapes, sizes, and weights. Project website: https://forceband-emg.github.io

</details>
